# Plugin System Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Dexo 1.5.0 downloads drivers and theme packs as signed, versioned plugins. DuckDB leaves the Dexo binary and ships only as a plugin.

**Architecture:**
- A plugin is a declarative package: `dexo-plugin.toml` plus files.
- A driver plugin is a separate executable. It speaks JSON-RPC 2.0 as one JSON message per line over stdin and stdout.
  - The protocol is defined once, in `dexo-plugin-protocol`.
  - The plugin side is served by `dexo-plugin-sdk::serve`.
- Inside Dexo, a `PluginFactory` (in `dexo-app::plugin`) implements `ConnectionFactory`/`Session` by forwarding to that process. It registers in the now-shared `DriverRegistry`, so the TUI, the CLI and MCP do not change.
- A minisign-signed index on a fixed GitHub release lists versions and per-target artifacts.
- Install order: signature → size/sha256 → safe extraction → handshake → atomic rename → `state.json`.

**Tech Stack:**
- Rust 2024 (MSRV 1.93), tokio, serde/serde_json.
- New crates: ureq 3.4 (rustls with the platform verifier), flate2, tar, minisign-verify; minisign as a test-only dependency.
- ratatui/crossterm, clap, GitHub Actions, a Python 3.12 index script.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-plugin-system-design.md` — read it first; this plan argues from it.

## Global Constraints

**CI gates and repository rules**
- CI gates run before every commit:
  - `cargo fmt --all`
  - `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`
  - The tests the task names
  - `cargo deny check` whenever `Cargo.toml` changes
- New dependencies come only from crates.io and must pass `cargo deny check`. The new ones:
  - In `dexo-app`: `ureq = { version = "3.4", default-features = false, features = ["rustls-no-provider", "platform-verifier"] }`, `rustls = "0.23.43"`, `flate2 = "1.1"`, `tar = "0.4.46"`, `minisign-verify = "0.2"`, `chrono.workspace = true`.
  - In `dexo-driver-api`: `base64.workspace = true`.
  - Test-only: `minisign = "0.8"`, in `dexo-test-support`.
- Crates with a binary that must not be released by cargo-dist get `[package.metadata.dist] dist = false`. `.github/workflows/release.yml` is generated by cargo-dist; never edit it by hand.

**Versions**
- Protocol version is `1`, and so are the manifest `schema` and the index `schema`.

**Limits (verbatim from the spec)**
- Handshake reply within 10 seconds.
- Exit 60 seconds after the last session closes.
- A cancel must be answered within 5 seconds, else the process is killed.
- At most 3 restarts a minute.
- stderr keeps its last 20 lines.
- A line over 64 MB is a protocol error.
- A page holds up to 500 rows or 4 MB.
- Downloads are capped at 256 MB.
- The index cache lasts 24 hours.
- A lock older than 10 minutes is stale.

**Identity and platform**
- Plugin id pattern: `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$`.
- Plugin process environment: only `PATH`, `HOME` (`USERPROFILE` on Windows), `TMPDIR`/`TMP`/`TEMP`, `LANG`/`LANGUAGE`/`LC_*`, and `SystemRoot`.
  - `SystemRoot` is added to the spec's list because Windows programs fail without it.
  - No `DEXO_*` variable is passed.
- Secrets never go on argv, the environment, logs, SQLite or TOML. Dexo never logs protocol message bodies, and `SessionOpenParams`'s `Debug` redacts secrets.

**Layering**
- The TUI, CLI and MCP go through `dexo-app`.
- Drivers never import UI crates.
- `dexo-plugin-protocol` and `dexo-plugin-sdk` never depend on `dexo-app`.

**Paths and environment variables**
- Everything lives under `AppPaths.data_dir/plugins/`, which honours `DEXO_DATA_HOME`.
- Any test or script that runs a dev `dexo` binary sets `DEXO_DATA_HOME`, `HOME` and `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` to a temp dir.
- Environment variables:
  - `DEXO_PLUGIN_INDEX` — the index URL; the signature is still required.
  - `DEXO_PLUGIN_TRUSTED_KEY` — one more trusted minisign public key, in base64.
  - `DEXO_DRIVER_<ID>` — a local executable for plugin `<ID>`, uppercased with `-` written as `_`.
- Tests never call `std::env::set_var` (it is `unsafe` in edition 2024 and racy). Library APIs take explicit values (`IndexSource`); only child processes get environment variables.

**Targets and URLs**
- Artifact keys:
  - `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`
  - `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`
  - `x86_64-apple-darwin`
  - `aarch64-apple-darwin`
  - `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`
  - `any` for a theme pack
- Default index: `https://github.com/KingDasWinx/Dexo/releases/download/plugins-index/index.json`. Its signature is the same URL plus `.minisig`.

**Copy, verbatim from the spec**
- `DuckDB is a plugin: install it with \`dexo plugin install duckdb\` or from Plugins (Ctrl+G p)`
- `the index signature does not match: nothing was installed`
- `offline: showing the index from <YYYY-MM-DD>`
- Trust prompt for a driver: `this program will receive the passwords of the connections that use it`
- Process failure: `the <Name> plugin stopped: <last log line>`

**Process rules**
- Code, comments, commits and docs are in English. Comments are short and say *why*, like the surrounding code.
- Work and commit on `development`, one commit per step marked *Commit*, ending with the attribution line the session uses.
- Never push.
- Never commit `.claude/` or `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-23-mcp-hardening-and-rewrite.md`.
- Never run `git stash`.
- Never touch the docker container `pg-commerce-lab`.

## Review Focus

1. **A plugin process killed between two queries** (crash, OOM, `kill -9`): the next call on an open session fails with `the DuckDB plugin stopped: <last log line>`, and a new connection restarts the process and works.
   → Task 8, `a_killed_plugin_fails_open_sessions_and_restarts`.
2. **Two sessions on one plugin, one reading a large result page by page**: the other session's small query answers while the first result is still open and undrained.
   → Task 4, `a_long_result_does_not_hold_up_another_session`.
3. **An update that fails late** (handshake refused, rename refused): the installed version keeps working, `state.json` still names it, and `plugins/.staging/` is left empty.
   → Task 11, `a_failed_update_keeps_the_previous_version`.
4. **A truncated, empty or tampered `index-cache.json`**: it is ignored and the index is fetched again; nothing panics.
   → Task 14, `a_corrupt_cache_is_fetched_again`.
5. **Removing (or disabling) a driver plugin while one of its sessions is open**: the session's next call fails with `the <Name> plugin stopped: it was removed`, nothing crashes, and connecting again says the driver is a plugin to install.
   → Task 11, `removing_a_plugin_ends_its_sessions_cleanly`.

## Where this plan refines the spec

These are deliberate. Each is one line in the docs (`docs/src/plugins.md`), not a change of design.

- **Params on a session** travel as `{"session": "s1", "args": …}`: one envelope for every family. The spec's examples show them flat. `QueryRequest` travels as `QueryExecute`, with `timeout_ms`, instead of a serde derive on `QueryRequest` itself.
- **`SystemRoot`** is added to the plugin's environment on Windows, where a program cannot start without it.
- **The offer to install a missing driver** comes on Test, Save, or opening a saved connection. It does not come the moment the driver is stepped to: that would pop a dialog while someone steps past DuckDB on the way to another driver.
- **"Plugins" in the palette** is *Go to Plugins*, as every other screen's entry is named.
- **The manifest's `dialect` and `url_schemes`** are validated and listed but not consumed in v1. Dexo's dialect and URL handling key on the driver id, which is right for DuckDB. A third-party driver with a known dialect under another id gets generic SQL handling until one needs more.

## File structure

New crates:

```
crates/dexo-plugin-protocol/      wire types, method names, framing (both sides)
  src/lib.rs                      constants, envelope, error codes, read_line/write_line
  src/messages.rs                 params and results of every method
  tests/protocol.rs
crates/dexo-plugin-sdk/           plugin side: serve any ConnectionFactory over stdio
  src/lib.rs                      serve, serve_io, serve_in_process, sessions, cursors, paging
  src/families.rs                 catalog/data/bulk/explain/tx dispatch
  tests/serve.rs
crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/       a misbehaving SQLite plugin + process/install tests
  src/main.rs
  tests/process.rs
  tests/install.rs
```

`dexo-app` gains `src/plugin/`:

```
mod.rs       plugins_dir, register/unregister, register_installed, register_dev_overrides, theme_dirs
rpc.rs       RpcClient: requests out, replies matched by id, on two std threads
host.rs      PluginHost: process lifecycle, handshake, restart budget, idle exit, Spawner, process_spawner
proxy.rs     PluginFactory + ProxySession implementing every protocol-1 trait
manifest.rs  dexo-plugin.toml parsing and validation
archive.rs   safe .tar.gz extraction
state.rs     state.json, Lock
install.rs   stage, commit, remove, set_enabled, install_from_index, upgrade, doctor
index.rs     IndexSource, Index, verify, load (cache/offline), choose, updates
http.rs      ureq download with cap, progress and cancel
keys.rs      trusted minisign public keys
```

Elsewhere:

```
crates/dexo-cli/src/plugin.rs              `dexo plugin …`
crates/dexo-tui/src/screens/plugins.rs     Plugins screen state + keys, plugin offer
crates/dexo-tui/src/screen/plugins.rs      Plugins screen drawing
crates/dexo-tui/src/runtime/plugin_manager.rs  effects → dexo-app plugin calls
crates/dexo-test-support/src/plugins.rs    TestKey (minisign), HttpServer, theme_pack, index helpers
plugins/duckdb/{dexo-plugin.toml,README.md}
plugins/extra-themes/{dexo-plugin.toml,README.md,themes/*.toml}
scripts/pack-plugin.sh  scripts/plugin-index.py  publish-plugin.sh
.github/workflows/plugins-release.yml
docs/src/plugins.md
```

`crates/dexo-driver-api/tests/wire.rs`:

```rust
//! The types a driver plugin sends Dexo travel as JSON: every one comes back as it left.
use dexo_driver_api::{
    Capability, CapabilityState, CatalogListOptions, ColumnId, ColumnMeta, ConnectionSecrets,
    DataRequest, DbValue, DriverError, DriverErrorCategory, ExplainRequest, Filter, Mutation, Page,
    QualifiedName, QueryEvent, RawClauses, RowBatch, Sort, TransactionMode, TransactionState,
};
use serde_json::json;

fn round_trip<T>(value: T) -> serde_json::Value
where
    T: serde::Serialize + serde::de::DeserializeOwned + PartialEq + std::fmt::Debug,
{
    let json = serde_json::to_value(&value).unwrap();
    let back: T = serde_json::from_value(json.clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back, value);
    json
}

#[test]
fn values_are_tagged_and_bytes_are_base64() {
    assert_eq!(round_trip(DbValue::I64(1)), json!({"t": "i64", "v": 1}));
    assert_eq!(round_trip(DbValue::Null), json!({"t": "null"}));
    assert_eq!(
        round_trip(DbValue::Bytes(vec![0, 255, 10])),
        json!({"t": "bytes", "v": "AP8K"})
    );
    round_trip(DbValue::U64(u64::MAX));
    round_trip(DbValue::Decimal("12345678901234567890.123".into()));
    round_trip(DbValue::Json(r#"{"a":[1,2]}"#.into()));
    round_trip(DbValue::Native {
        type_name: "interval".into(),
        bytes: vec![1, 2],
        text: "1 day".into(),
    });
}

#[test]
fn events_and_requests_round_trip() {
    round_trip(QueryEvent::Columns(vec![ColumnMeta {
        name: "id".into(),
        type_name: "int8".into(),
        nullable: false,
    }]));
    assert_eq!(
        round_trip(QueryEvent::Rows(RowBatch {
            rows: vec![vec![DbValue::I64(1)]]
        })),
        json!({"rows": [[{"t": "i64", "v": 1}]]})
    );
    round_trip(QueryEvent::ResultSetStarted { index: 0 });
    round_trip(QueryEvent::ResultSetFinished {
        index: 0,
        rows_affected: Some(3),
        truncated: true,
    });
    round_trip(QueryEvent::Notice {
        message: "hi".into(),
    });
    round_trip(QueryEvent::Finished {
        rows_affected: None,
    });
    round_trip(CapabilityState::unavailable(Capability::Ddl, "no DDL"));
    round_trip(CapabilityState::available(Capability::Query));
    round_trip(TransactionState::Failed);
    round_trip(TransactionMode::ReadOnly);
    round_trip(CatalogListOptions {
        include_system: true,
    });
    round_trip(ExplainRequest::with_indexes(
        "select 1",
        vec!["create index on t (a)".into()],
    ));
    round_trip(Mutation::Update {
        table: QualifiedName::new(None::<String>, Some("main"), "t"),
        identity: vec![(ColumnId("id".into()), DbValue::I64(1))],
        original: vec![],
        changes: vec![(ColumnId("name".into()), DbValue::Text("x".into()))],
    });
    round_trip(DataRequest {
        object: QualifiedName::new(None::<String>, None::<String>, "t"),
        columns: vec![],
        filter: Some(Filter::Not(Box::new(Filter::IsNull(ColumnId("a".into()))))),
        sort: vec![Sort {
            column: ColumnId("a".into()),
            descending: true,
        }],
        page: Page::new(0, 50).unwrap(),
        clauses: RawClauses::default(),
    });
}

#[test]
fn a_driver_error_keeps_every_field() {
    let error = DriverError::new(DriverErrorCategory::Syntax, "syntax error at or near \"selec\"")
        .with_native_code("42601")
        .with_position(1)
        .with_row(2)
        .with_detail("d")
        .with_hint("h")
        .retryable();
    let back: DriverError =
        serde_json::from_value(serde_json::to_value(&error).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back.to_string(), error.to_string());
    assert_eq!(back.category(), DriverErrorCategory::Syntax);
    assert_eq!(back.native_code(), Some("42601"));
    assert_eq!((back.position(), back.row()), (Some(1), Some(2)));
    assert_eq!(
        (back.detail(), back.hint(), back.is_retryable()),
        (Some("d"), Some("h"), true)
    );
}

#[test]
fn secrets_list_their_purposes() {
    let mut secrets = ConnectionSecrets::database_password("pw".to_string().into());
    secrets.insert("ssh_passphrase", "pp".to_string().into());
    let purposes: Vec<&str> = secrets.iter().map(|(purpose, _)| purpose).collect();
    assert_eq!(purposes, ["database_password", "ssh_passphrase"]);
}
```

`Cargo.toml` — add under `[dependencies]`:

```toml
base64.workspace = true
```

`src/base64_bytes.rs`:

```rust
//! `Vec<u8>` as one base64 string in JSON: a byte array spelled out as numbers is four
//! times its size on the wire.

use base64::Engine;
use base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD;
use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serializer};

pub fn serialize<S: Serializer>(bytes: &[u8], serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
    serializer.serialize_str(&STANDARD.encode(bytes))
}

pub fn deserialize<'de, D: Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Vec<u8>, D::Error> {
    let text = String::deserialize(deserializer)?;
    STANDARD.decode(text).map_err(serde::de::Error::custom)
}
```

`src/lib.rs` — add `pub mod base64_bytes;` above `mod admin;`.

`src/value.rs` — replace the derive and the enum header:

```rust
/// A value as drivers hand it over. On the wire, `{"t": "i64", "v": 1}`: tagged, so a
/// decimal or a JSON document never turns into a float or an object on the way.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "t", content = "v", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum DbValue {
    Null,
    Bool(bool),
    I64(i64),
    U64(u64),
    Decimal(String),
    Text(String),
    Bytes(#[serde(with = "crate::base64_bytes")] Vec<u8>),
    Json(String),
    Native {
        type_name: String,
        #[serde(with = "crate::base64_bytes")]
        bytes: Vec<u8>,
        text: String,
    },
}
```

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum DriverErrorCategory { /* unchanged */ }

/// Serializable so a driver plugin's error reaches Dexo with every field: a plugin's
/// syntax error points at its position like a built-in driver's.
#[derive(Debug, Error, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
#[error("{message}")]
pub struct DriverError {
    category: DriverErrorCategory,
    message: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    native_code: Option<String>,
    /// (keep the existing doc comment)
    #[serde(default)]
    position: Option<u32>,
    /// (keep the existing doc comment)
    #[serde(default)]
    row: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    detail: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    hint: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    retryable: bool,
}
```

### Task 2: Owned descriptors, announced drivers, a shared registry

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dexo-driver-api/src/connection.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dexo-driver-api/tests/contracts.rs:37-50`
- Modify: `crates/dexo-app/src/driver_registry.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dexo-app/src/session_manager.rs:121-135`, `crates/dexo-tui/src/runtime/mod.rs:3134-3147` (struct literals)
- Modify: `crates/dexo-tui/src/screens/connection.rs:360-380,575-585,822-858,940,1321`
- Modify: `crates/dexo-tui/src/screens/connections.rs:520-522`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `DriverDescriptor` fields: `id: Cow<'static, str>`, `display_name: Cow<'static, str>`, `default_port: u16`, `options`, `file: bool`, `plugin: Option<Cow<'static, str>>`, `installed: bool`.
  - `DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver(id: impl Into<String>, display_name: impl Into<String>, file: bool, plugin: impl Into<String>, installed: bool) -> Self`.
  - `DriverDescriptor::announce(Self)`, `DriverDescriptor::withdraw(&str)`, `DriverDescriptor::known() -> Vec<Self>`, `DriverDescriptor::for_id(&str) -> Option<Self>` (built-ins + announced).
  - `ConnectionFactory::driver_name(&self) -> String`.
  - `DriverRegistry` is now `Clone` with a shared table:
    - `register(&mut self, Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>)` (unchanged)
    - `insert(&self, Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>)`
    - `remove(&self, &str) -> Option<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>`
    - `has(&self, &str) -> bool`
    - `get` and `descriptors` as before.
  - Inserting a factory whose descriptor has `plugin: Some(_)` announces it `installed: true`; removing it announces it `installed: false`.
  - `DriverDescriptor::duckdb()` has `plugin: Some("duckdb")` and `installed: false`.

```rust
    #[test]
    fn an_announced_driver_is_known_and_replaces_its_earlier_self() {
        assert!(!DriverDescriptor::for_id("duckdb").unwrap().installed);
        assert!(DriverDescriptor::for_id("postgres").unwrap().installed);
        let id = "announce-test";
        DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver(
            id,
            "Announce Test",
            true,
            "announce-test",
            false,
        ));
        let found = DriverDescriptor::for_id(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(found.display_name, "Announce Test");
        assert!(found.file && !found.installed);
        assert_eq!(found.plugin.as_deref(), Some("announce-test"));
        DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver(
            id,
            "Announce Test",
            true,
            "announce-test",
            true,
        ));
        assert!(DriverDescriptor::for_id(id).unwrap().installed);
        assert_eq!(
            DriverDescriptor::known()
                .iter()
                .filter(|known| known.id == id)
                .count(),
            1
        );
        DriverDescriptor::withdraw(id);
        assert!(DriverDescriptor::for_id(id).is_none());
    }
```

```rust
    fn a_plugin_driver_not_installed_says_how_to_get_it() {
        let registry = DriverRegistry::new();
        let missing = registry.get("duckdb").err().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(missing.category(), ErrorCategory::Capability);
        assert!(
            missing.to_string().contains(
                "DuckDB is a plugin: install it with `dexo plugin install duckdb` or from Plugins (Ctrl+G p)"
            ),
            "{missing}"
        );
        let unknown = registry.get("oracle").err().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unknown.category(), ErrorCategory::Configuration);
        // Postgres is built in: a registry without it simply lacks it.
        let unregistered = registry.get("postgres").err().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unregistered.category(), ErrorCategory::Configuration);
    }

    #[test]
    fn clones_share_their_drivers_and_announce_them() {
        let registry = DriverRegistry::new();
        let clone = registry.clone();
        clone.insert(Arc::new(Fake));
        assert!(registry.get("registry-fake").is_ok());
        assert!(DriverDescriptor::for_id("registry-fake").unwrap().installed);
        assert!(registry.remove("registry-fake").is_some());
        assert!(!clone.has("registry-fake"));
        assert!(!DriverDescriptor::for_id("registry-fake").unwrap().installed);
        DriverDescriptor::withdraw("registry-fake");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the descriptor**

In `crates/dexo-driver-api/src/connection.rs`:

```rust
use std::borrow::Cow;
use std::sync::{PoisonError, RwLock};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub struct DriverDescriptor {
    pub id: Cow<'static, str>,
    pub display_name: Cow<'static, str>,
    pub default_port: u16,
    pub options: ConnectionOptions,
    /// Opens a file instead of dialling a host: no port, user, password or transport.
    pub file: bool,
    /// The plugin that brings the driver, for one Dexo does not build in.
    pub plugin: Option<Cow<'static, str>>,
    /// Whether it can connect now: a plugin's driver is known before it is installed,
    /// so the connection form can offer to install it.
    pub installed: bool,
}

/// Drivers made known while Dexo runs: installed plugins, and the index's drivers that
/// are not installed.
static ANNOUNCED: RwLock<Vec<DriverDescriptor>> = RwLock::new(Vec::new());
```

In each built-in constructor, use `.into()` on the string fields and set the new fields:

```rust

        Self {
            id: "postgres".into(),
            display_name: "PostgreSQL".into(),
            default_port: 5432,
            options: ConnectionOptions {
                tls: true,
                client_certificate: true,
                ssh: true,
                proxy: true,
            },
            file: false,
            plugin: None,
            installed: true,
        }
    }
```

Treat `mysql()` and `sqlite()` the same way; `mariadb()` keeps `..Self::mysql()`. DuckDB:

```rust
    /// A DuckDB file, `:memory:`, or a CSV, Parquet or JSON file read as a table. Its
    /// engine is a plugin: Dexo keeps its dialect and its guard, the plugin brings it.
    pub fn duckdb() -> Self {
        Self {
            id: "duckdb".into(),
            display_name: "DuckDB".into(),
            plugin: Some("duckdb".into()),
            installed: false,
            ..Self::sqlite()
        }
    }

    /// A driver a plugin brings, as its manifest or the index describes it.
    pub fn plugin_driver(
        id: impl Into<String>,
        display_name: impl Into<String>,
        file: bool,
        plugin: impl Into<String>,
        installed: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: Cow::Owned(id.into()),
            display_name: Cow::Owned(display_name.into()),
            default_port: 0,
            options: ConnectionOptions {
                tls: false,
                client_certificate: false,
                ssh: false,
                proxy: false,
            },
            file,
            plugin: Some(Cow::Owned(plugin.into())),
            installed,
        }
    }

    /// Makes `descriptor` known, in place of a built-in or an earlier announcement with
    /// its id.
    pub fn announce(descriptor: Self) {
        let mut announced = ANNOUNCED.write().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner);
        announced.retain(|known| known.id != descriptor.id);
        announced.push(descriptor);
    }

    pub fn withdraw(id: &str) {
        ANNOUNCED
            .write()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .retain(|known| known.id != id);
    }

    /// Every driver Dexo knows: the built-in ones in the order the form lists them, each
    /// as announced when it was, then the other announced ones by name.
    pub fn known() -> Vec<Self> {
        let announced = ANNOUNCED
            .read()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .clone();
        let mut known: Vec<Self> = [
            Self::postgres(),
            Self::mysql(),
            Self::mariadb(),
            Self::sqlite(),
            Self::duckdb(),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .map(|builtin| {
            announced
                .iter()
                .find(|found| found.id == builtin.id)
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or(builtin)
        })
        .collect();
        let mut rest: Vec<Self> = announced
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|found| !known.iter().any(|builtin| builtin.id == found.id))
            .collect();
        rest.sort_by(|a, b| a.display_name.cmp(&b.display_name));
        known.extend(rest);
        known
    }

    pub fn for_id(id: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Self::known()
            .into_iter()
            .find(|descriptor| descriptor.id == id)
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the registry**

Replace the body of `crates/dexo-app/src/driver_registry.rs` above its tests:

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, PoisonError, RwLock};

use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectionFactory, DriverDescriptor};

use crate::error::{AppError, ErrorCategory};

/// The drivers Dexo connects with. Clones share one table: a driver plugin installed
/// from the Plugins screen is there for every part of Dexo without a restart.
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub struct DriverRegistry {
    factories: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>>>,
}

impl DriverRegistry {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn register(&mut self, factory: Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>) {
        self.insert(factory);
    }

    /// Adds a driver; a plugin's is announced as installed, so the forms list it so.
    pub fn insert(&self, factory: Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>) {
        let descriptor = factory.descriptor();
        if descriptor.plugin.is_some() {
            DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor {
                installed: true,
                ..descriptor.clone()
            });
        }
        self.factories
            .write()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .insert(descriptor.id.into_owned(), factory);
    }

    /// Takes a driver out; a plugin's stays known, as not installed.
    pub fn remove(&self, driver: &str) -> Option<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>> {
        let removed = self
            .factories
            .write()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .remove(driver)?;
        let descriptor = removed.descriptor();
        if descriptor.plugin.is_some() {
            DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor {
                installed: false,
                ..descriptor
            });
        }
        Some(removed)
    }

    pub fn has(&self, driver: &str) -> bool {
        self.factories
            .read()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .contains_key(driver)
    }

    /// A plugin's driver that is not installed says how to get it.
    pub fn get(&self, driver: &str) -> Result<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>, AppError> {
        if let Some(factory) = self
            .factories
            .read()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .get(driver)
        {
            return Ok(factory.clone());
        }
        Err(
            match DriverDescriptor::for_id(driver).and_then(|descriptor| {
                let plugin = descriptor.plugin.clone()?;
                Some((descriptor, plugin))
            }) {
                Some((descriptor, plugin)) => AppError::new(
                    ErrorCategory::Capability,
                    format!(
                        "{} is a plugin: install it with `dexo plugin install {plugin}` or from Plugins (Ctrl+G p)",
                        descriptor.display_name
                    ),
                ),
                None => AppError::new(
                    ErrorCategory::Configuration,
                    format!("unknown driver '{driver}'"),
                ),
            },
        )
    }

    pub fn descriptors(&self) -> Vec<DriverDescriptor> {
        let mut descriptors: Vec<_> = self
            .factories
            .read()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .values()
            .map(|factory| factory.descriptor())
            .collect();
        descriptors.sort_by(|a, b| a.id.cmp(&b.id));
        descriptors
    }
}
```

`FEATURE_GATED` goes away. `dexo-app` already has `async-trait` as a dependency, which the test's `Fake` uses.

```rust
/// The drivers the form steps through: every one Dexo knows, a plugin's whether or not
/// it is installed -- trying one that is not offers to install it.
fn drivers() -> Vec<String> {
    DriverDescriptor::known()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|descriptor| descriptor.id.into_owned())
        .collect()
}

/// A driver Dexo knows, or Postgres for an id it does not.
fn normalize_driver(driver: &str) -> String {
    DriverDescriptor::for_id(driver.trim()).map_or_else(
        || DriverDescriptor::postgres().id.into_owned(),
        |descriptor| descriptor.id.into_owned(),
    )
}

fn next_driver(current: &str, delta: i32) -> String {
    let known = drivers();
    let index = known
        .iter()
        .position(|id| id == current.trim())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let next = (index as i32 + delta).rem_euclid(known.len() as i32) as usize;
    known[next].clone()
}
```

```rust
use std::io::BufReader;

use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectRequest, DriverError, DriverErrorCategory, QueryRequest};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{
    Family, QueryExecute, Request, RpcError, SessionOpenParams, SessionOpenResult, code,
    read_line, read_line_with, write_line,
};
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};

#[test]
fn messages_are_one_per_line() {
    let mut wire = Vec::new();
    write_line(&mut wire, &Request::new(1, "initialize", ()).unwrap()).unwrap();
    write_line(&mut wire, &Request::new(2, "session/close", ()).unwrap()).unwrap();
    wire.extend_from_slice(b"{\"last\":true}");
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(&wire[..]);
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    assert!(read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap());
    let first: Request = serde_json::from_slice(&line).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((first.id, first.method.as_str()), (1, "initialize"));
    assert!(read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap());
    assert!(read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(line, b"{\"last\":true}");
    assert!(!read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap());
}

#[test]
fn a_line_past_the_cap_is_refused() {
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(&b"0123456789\n"[..]);
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    let error = read_line_with(&mut reader, &mut line, 8).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(error.kind(), std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData);
}

#[test]
fn opening_a_session_carries_the_secrets_and_never_prints_them() {
    let mut request = ConnectRequest::new(
        "db.example:5432",
        Some("shop".into()),
        "ada".into(),
        SecretString::from("hunter2".to_string()),
        true,
    );
    request
        .secrets
        .insert("ssh_passphrase", SecretString::from("s3cr3t-pp".to_string()));
    let params = SessionOpenParams::new("postgres", &request);
    let debug = format!("{params:?}");
    assert!(!debug.contains("hunter2") && !debug.contains("s3cr3t"), "{debug}");
    let json = serde_json::to_string(&params).unwrap();
    let back: SessionOpenParams = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back.driver, "postgres");
    let restored = back.into_request();
    assert_eq!(restored.secret.expose_secret(), "hunter2");
    assert_eq!(
        restored.secrets.get("ssh_passphrase").unwrap().expose_secret(),
        "s3cr3t-pp"
    );
    assert_eq!(restored.transport, request.transport);
    assert_eq!(restored.database.as_deref(), Some("shop"));
    assert!(restored.read_only);
}

#[test]
fn a_driver_error_crosses_the_wire_whole() {
    let error = DriverError::new(DriverErrorCategory::Syntax, "near \"selec\": syntax error")
        .with_position(1);
    let back = RpcError::driver(&error).into_driver_error();
    assert_eq!(back.category(), DriverErrorCategory::Syntax);
    assert_eq!(back.position(), Some(1));
    let other = RpcError::new(code::INTERNAL, "boom").into_driver_error();
    assert_eq!(other.category(), DriverErrorCategory::Internal);
    assert_eq!(other.to_string(), "boom");
}

#[test]
fn what_a_newer_plugin_adds_is_skipped() {
    let json = r#"{"session":"s1",
        "capabilities":[{"capability":"query","available":true},{"capability":"teleport","available":true}],
        "families":["catalog","teleport"]}"#;
    let opened: SessionOpenResult = serde_json::from_str(json).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(opened.families, [Family::Catalog]);
    assert_eq!(opened.capabilities.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(opened.transaction, None);
}

#[test]
fn a_query_request_keeps_its_timeout_and_flags() {
    let mut request = QueryRequest::read("select 1", 1000);
    request.timeout = std::time::Duration::from_millis(1500);
    request.read_only = true;
    let back = QueryExecute::new(&request).into_request();
    assert_eq!(back.id, request.id);
    assert_eq!(back.timeout, request.timeout);
    assert_eq!((back.row_limit, back.read_only, back.mutating), (1000, true, false));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/lib.rs`**

```rust
//! The protocol between Dexo and a driver plugin: JSON-RPC 2.0, one message per line,
//! over the plugin's stdin and stdout. Both sides use these types, so the two ends cannot
//! drift apart the way two copies of a wire format do.

mod messages;

pub use messages::*;

use std::io::{self, BufRead, Read, Write};

use dexo_driver_api::{DriverError, DriverErrorCategory};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;

/// The protocol version this build speaks. Version 1 is frozen once released: changes
/// come as new families, which a session negotiates, or as version 2.
pub const PROTOCOL: u32 = 1;
/// A longer line is a protocol error, not a reason to run out of memory.
pub const MAX_LINE: usize = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
/// A page of a result holds at most this many rows...
pub const PAGE_ROWS: usize = 500;
/// ...or about this many bytes of values, whichever comes first.
pub const PAGE_BYTES: usize = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

pub mod method {
    pub const INITIALIZE: &str = "initialize";
    pub const SESSION_OPEN: &str = "session/open";
    pub const SESSION_CLOSE: &str = "session/close";
    pub const QUERY_EXECUTE: &str = "query/execute";
    pub const QUERY_FETCH: &str = "query/fetch";
    pub const QUERY_CLOSE: &str = "query/close";
    pub const QUERY_CANCEL: &str = "query/cancel";
    pub const CATALOG_LIST_CHILDREN: &str = "catalog/list_children";
    pub const CATALOG_OBJECT: &str = "catalog/object";
    pub const CATALOG_DDL: &str = "catalog/ddl";
    pub const CATALOG_DEPENDENCIES: &str = "catalog/dependencies";
    pub const CATALOG_DEPENDENTS: &str = "catalog/dependents";
    pub const CATALOG_FOREIGN_KEYS: &str = "catalog/foreign_keys";
    pub const CATALOG_RELATIONS_NAMED: &str = "catalog/relations_named";
    pub const CATALOG_DATABASES: &str = "catalog/databases";
    pub const DATA_FETCH: &str = "data/fetch";
    pub const DATA_FETCH_VALUE: &str = "data/fetch_value";
    pub const DATA_APPLY: &str = "data/apply";
    pub const DATA_TABLE_COLUMNS: &str = "data/table_columns";
    pub const DATA_ESTIMATE_ROWS: &str = "data/estimate_rows";
    pub const BULK_INSERT_BATCH: &str = "bulk/insert_batch";
    pub const EXPLAIN: &str = "explain/explain";
    pub const TX_BEGIN: &str = "tx/begin";
    pub const TX_COMMIT: &str = "tx/commit";
    pub const TX_ROLLBACK: &str = "tx/rollback";
    pub const TX_SAVEPOINT: &str = "tx/savepoint";
    pub const TX_ROLLBACK_TO: &str = "tx/rollback_to";
    pub const TX_RELEASE_SAVEPOINT: &str = "tx/release_savepoint";
}

/// JSON-RPC's error codes, then the protocol's own.
pub mod code {
    pub const PARSE_ERROR: i64 = -32700;
    pub const INVALID_REQUEST: i64 = -32600;
    pub const METHOD_NOT_FOUND: i64 = -32601;
    pub const INVALID_PARAMS: i64 = -32602;
    pub const INTERNAL: i64 = -32603;
    /// The driver failed; `data` is the `DriverError`, every field of it.
    pub const DRIVER: i64 = 1;
    pub const UNKNOWN_SESSION: i64 = 2;
    pub const UNKNOWN_CURSOR: i64 = 3;
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Request {
    pub jsonrpc: String,
    pub id: u64,
    pub method: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub params: Value,
}

impl Request {
    pub fn new(id: u64, method: &str, params: impl Serialize) -> serde_json::Result<Self> {
        Ok(Self {
            jsonrpc: "2.0".into(),
            id,
            method: method.into(),
            params: serde_json::to_value(params)?,
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Response {
    pub jsonrpc: String,
    pub id: u64,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub result: Option<Value>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<RpcError>,
}

impl Response {
    pub fn ok(id: u64, result: Value) -> Self {
        Self {
            jsonrpc: "2.0".into(),
            id,
            result: Some(result),
            error: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn err(id: u64, error: RpcError) -> Self {
        Self {
            jsonrpc: "2.0".into(),
            id,
            result: None,
            error: Some(error),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RpcError {
    pub code: i64,
    pub message: String,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub data: Option<Value>,
}

impl RpcError {
    pub fn new(code: i64, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            code,
            message: message.into(),
            data: None,
        }
    }

    /// A driver's error, every field of it in `data`.
    pub fn driver(error: &DriverError) -> Self {
        Self {
            code: code::DRIVER,
            message: error.to_string(),
            data: serde_json::to_value(error).ok(),
        }
    }

    /// The driver's error this carries, or one made of its message.
    pub fn into_driver_error(self) -> DriverError {
        if self.code == code::DRIVER
            && let Some(error) = self
                .data
                .and_then(|data| serde_json::from_value::<DriverError>(data).ok())
        {
            return error;
        }
        DriverError::new(DriverErrorCategory::Internal, self.message)
    }
}

/// Reads one message line into `line`, its line ending dropped. `Ok(false)` at the end
/// of input; a line over [`MAX_LINE`] is `InvalidData`.
pub fn read_line(reader: &mut impl BufRead, line: &mut Vec<u8>) -> io::Result<bool> {
    read_line_with(reader, line, MAX_LINE)
}

/// [`read_line`] with a cap of `max` bytes.
pub fn read_line_with(
    reader: &mut impl BufRead,
    line: &mut Vec<u8>,
    max: usize,
) -> io::Result<bool> {
    line.clear();
    let read = reader.by_ref().take(max as u64 + 1).read_until(b'\n', line)?;
    if read == 0 {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    if line.last() == Some(&b'\n') {
        line.pop();
    } else if line.len() > max {
        return Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
            "a message is longer than the protocol allows",
        ));
    }
    if line.last() == Some(&b'\r') {
        line.pop();
    }
    Ok(true)
}

/// Writes `message` as one line and flushes it.
pub fn write_line(writer: &mut impl Write, message: &impl Serialize) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec(message).map_err(io::Error::other)?;
    bytes.push(b'\n');
    writer.write_all(&bytes)?;
    writer.flush()
}
```

```rust
//! The params and results of every method.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fmt;
use std::time::Duration;

use dexo_driver_api::{
    CapabilityState, ConnectRequest, ConnectionSecrets, DbValue, DriverError, QueryEvent, QueryId,
    QueryRequest, TransactionState, TransportRequest,
};
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InitializeParams {
    pub dexo_version: String,
    pub protocols: Vec<u32>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InitializeResult {
    pub protocol: u32,
    pub driver_version: String,
    pub drivers: Vec<String>,
}

/// What `ConnectRequest` carries, secrets included: they travel only inside this
/// message on the plugin's stdin, never on its command line or in its environment.
#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SessionOpenParams {
    pub driver: String,
    pub endpoint: String,
    pub database: Option<String>,
    pub username: String,
    pub password: String,
    pub read_only: bool,
    pub transport: TransportRequest,
    pub secrets: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

impl fmt::Debug for SessionOpenParams {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("SessionOpenParams")
            .field("driver", &self.driver)
            .field("endpoint", &self.endpoint)
            .field("database", &self.database)
            .field("username", &self.username)
            .field("password", &"[REDACTED]")
            .field("read_only", &self.read_only)
            .field("secrets", &self.secrets.keys().collect::<Vec<_>>())
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl SessionOpenParams {
    pub fn new(driver: &str, request: &ConnectRequest) -> Self {
        Self {
            driver: driver.into(),
            endpoint: request.endpoint.clone(),
            database: request.database.clone(),
            username: request.username.clone(),
            password: request.secret.expose_secret().to_string(),
            read_only: request.read_only,
            transport: request.transport.clone(),
            secrets: request
                .secrets
                .iter()
                .map(|(purpose, secret)| (purpose.to_string(), secret.expose_secret().to_string()))
                .collect(),
        }
    }

    pub fn into_request(self) -> ConnectRequest {
        let mut secrets = ConnectionSecrets::default();
        for (purpose, secret) in self.secrets {
            secrets.insert(purpose, SecretString::from(secret));
        }
        ConnectRequest {
            endpoint: self.endpoint,
            database: self.database,
            username: self.username,
            secret: SecretString::from(self.password),
            read_only: self.read_only,
            transport: self.transport,
            secrets,
        }
    }
}

/// A group of methods a session answers beyond queries. Negotiated per session, so a
/// family added later needs no new protocol version.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Family {
    Catalog,
    Data,
    Bulk,
    Explain,
    Transactions,
    Cancel,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SessionOpenResult {
    pub session: String,
    #[serde(deserialize_with = "known_only")]
    pub capabilities: Vec<CapabilityState>,
    #[serde(deserialize_with = "known_only")]
    pub families: Vec<Family>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub server_session_id: Option<String>,
    /// The transaction state, for a session with the transactions family.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub transaction: Option<TransactionState>,
}

/// The entries this build knows. What a newer plugin adds -- a family, a capability --
/// is left out instead of failing the whole message.
fn known_only<'de, D, T>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Vec<T>, D::Error>
where
    D: Deserializer<'de>,
    T: DeserializeOwned,
{
    let values = Vec::<Value>::deserialize(deserializer)?;
    Ok(values
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|value| serde_json::from_value(value).ok())
        .collect())
}

/// A method's params on one session.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OnSession<T> {
    pub session: String,
    pub args: T,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct QueryExecute {
    pub query: Uuid,
    pub sql: String,
    pub params: Vec<DbValue>,
    pub row_limit: u64,
    pub timeout_ms: u64,
    pub read_only: bool,
    pub mutating: bool,
}

impl QueryExecute {
    pub fn new(request: &QueryRequest) -> Self {
        Self {
            query: request.id.0,
            sql: request.sql.clone(),
            params: request.parameters.clone(),
            row_limit: request.row_limit,
            timeout_ms: u64::try_from(request.timeout.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
            read_only: request.read_only,
            mutating: request.mutating,
        }
    }

    pub fn into_request(self) -> QueryRequest {
        QueryRequest {
            id: QueryId(self.query),
            sql: self.sql,
            parameters: self.params,
            row_limit: self.row_limit,
            timeout: Duration::from_millis(self.timeout_ms),
            mutating: self.mutating,
            read_only: self.read_only,
        }
    }
}

/// A page of a result. While `done` is false, `cursor` names the rest. The last page
/// carries the session's transaction state -- a statement may have changed it -- and
/// an error ends the result.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct QueryPage {
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub cursor: Option<String>,
    pub events: Vec<QueryEvent>,
    pub done: bool,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<DriverError>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub transaction: Option<TransactionState>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Cursor {
    pub cursor: String,
}

/// What a transaction method left: the state and notice the host keeps for the
/// synchronous `state()` and `take_notice()`, and the error when it failed -- after
/// which the state is still news.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TxResult {
    pub state: TransactionState,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub notice: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub error: Option<DriverError>,
}

/// Bytes as one base64 string.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Bytes(#[serde(with = "dexo_driver_api::base64_bytes")] pub Vec<u8>);
```

```rust
//! The SDK answers the protocol for any driver: SQLite here, over in-process pipes, the
//! test playing Dexo's part line by line.
use std::io::{BufReader, PipeReader, PipeWriter};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;

use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectRequest, DbValue, QueryEvent};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{
    QueryPage, Request, Response, SessionOpenParams, code, read_line, write_line,
};
use dexo_driver_sqlite::SqliteFactory;
use secrecy::SecretString;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

struct Wire {
    to: PipeWriter,
    from: BufReader<PipeReader>,
    next: u64,
}

impl Wire {
    fn start() -> Self {
        let (from, to) = dexo_plugin_sdk::serve_in_process(vec![Arc::new(SqliteFactory)]).unwrap();
        Self {
            to,
            from: BufReader::new(from),
            next: 0,
        }
    }

    fn call(&mut self, method: &str, params: Value) -> Response {
        self.next += 1;
        write_line(&mut self.to, &Request::new(self.next, method, params).unwrap()).unwrap();
        let mut line = Vec::new();
        assert!(read_line(&mut self.from, &mut line).unwrap(), "the plugin hung up");
        serde_json::from_slice(&line).unwrap()
    }

    fn ok(&mut self, method: &str, params: Value) -> Value {
        let response = self.call(method, params);
        assert!(response.error.is_none(), "{method}: {:?}", response.error);
        response.result.unwrap()
    }

    fn open(&mut self, path: &Path) -> String {
        let request = ConnectRequest::new(
            path.to_string_lossy(),
            None,
            String::new(),
            SecretString::from(String::new()),
            false,
        );
        let params = serde_json::to_value(SessionOpenParams::new("sqlite", &request)).unwrap();
        self.ok("session/open", params)["session"]
            .as_str()
            .unwrap()
            .to_string()
    }

    fn execute(&mut self, session: &str, sql: &str) -> QueryPage {
        let page = self.ok(
            "query/execute",
            json!({"session": session, "args": {
                "query": uuid::Uuid::new_v4(), "sql": sql, "params": [], "row_limit": 0,
                "timeout_ms": 30000, "read_only": false, "mutating": false}}),
        );
        serde_json::from_value(page).unwrap()
    }
}

const COUNT_TO_1200: &str = "WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i + 1 FROM n WHERE i < 1200) SELECT i FROM n";

fn rows_in(page: &QueryPage) -> usize {
    page.events
        .iter()
        .map(|event| match event {
            QueryEvent::Rows(batch) => batch.rows.len(),
            _ => 0,
        })
        .sum()
}

#[test]
fn the_handshake_names_the_drivers_and_refuses_another_protocol() {
    let mut wire = Wire::start();
    let ok = wire.ok("initialize", json!({"dexo_version": "1.5.0", "protocols": [1]}));
    assert_eq!(ok["protocol"], 1);
    assert_eq!(ok["drivers"], json!(["sqlite"]));
    let refused = wire.call("initialize", json!({"dexo_version": "9.0.0", "protocols": [2]}));
    assert_eq!(refused.error.unwrap().code, code::INVALID_REQUEST);
}

#[test]
fn a_result_comes_in_pages_of_500_rows() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut wire = Wire::start();
    let session = wire.open(&dir.path().join("a.db"));
    let first = wire.execute(&session, COUNT_TO_1200);
    assert_eq!(rows_in(&first), 500);
    assert!(!first.done);
    let cursor = first.cursor.unwrap();
    let fetch = |wire: &mut Wire| -> QueryPage {
        serde_json::from_value(wire.ok("query/fetch", json!({"cursor": cursor}))).unwrap()
    };
    assert_eq!(rows_in(&fetch(&mut wire)), 500);
    let last = fetch(&mut wire);
    assert_eq!(rows_in(&last), 200);
    assert!(last.done && last.cursor.is_none());
    assert!(last
        .events
        .iter()
        .any(|event| matches!(event, QueryEvent::Finished { .. })));
    let gone = wire.call("query/fetch", json!({"cursor": cursor}));
    assert_eq!(gone.error.unwrap().code, code::UNKNOWN_CURSOR);
}

/// Review focus 2: a result read page by page holds nothing up -- the driver only works
/// when asked for the next page, and every request is answered on its own.
#[test]
fn a_long_result_does_not_hold_up_another_session() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("a.db");
    let mut wire = Wire::start();
    let long = wire.open(&path);
    let short = wire.open(&path);
    let first = wire.execute(&long, COUNT_TO_1200);
    assert!(!first.done, "the long result is still open");
    let answer = wire.execute(&short, "SELECT 42");
    assert!(answer.done);
    let rows: Vec<Vec<DbValue>> = answer
        .events
        .into_iter()
        .flat_map(|event| match event {
            QueryEvent::Rows(batch) => batch.rows,
            _ => vec![],
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(rows, vec![vec![DbValue::I64(42)]]);
    wire.ok("query/close", json!({"cursor": first.cursor.unwrap()}));
}

#[test]
fn a_driver_error_comes_back_whole() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut wire = Wire::start();
    let session = wire.open(&dir.path().join("a.db"));
    let page = wire.execute(&session, "SELEC 1");
    let error = match page.error {
        Some(error) => error,
        None => panic!("no error: {page:?}"),
    };
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("syntax"), "{error}");
    let unknown = wire.call(
        "query/execute",
        json!({"session": "nope", "args": {"query": uuid::Uuid::new_v4(), "sql": "select 1",
            "params": [], "row_limit": 0, "timeout_ms": 1000, "read_only": false, "mutating": false}}),
    );
    assert_eq!(unknown.error.unwrap().code, code::UNKNOWN_SESSION);
}

#[test]
fn the_end_of_stdin_ends_the_server() {
    let wire = Wire::start();
    let Wire { to, mut from, .. } = wire;
    drop(to);
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    // The server's end of the pipe closes once it has returned.
    assert!(!read_line(&mut from, &mut line).unwrap());
}
```

Notes:
- `uuid` is used through the SDK's dependency, so add `uuid.workspace = true` to `[dev-dependencies]` too.
- `execute` may answer `SELEC 1` either with an error *page* (`page.error`) or with a JSON-RPC error. The SQLite driver fails while reading, so it is a page. If the driver fails in `execute` itself, the SDK answers a JSON-RPC error instead. The helper `ok()` would then panic, so the test uses `page.error` only because SQLite fails late. If it fails early on this machine, change the test to `wire.call(...)` and check `error.code == code::DRIVER`.

```rust
//! Turns any `ConnectionFactory` into a driver plugin. `serve` answers the plugin
//! protocol on stdin and stdout until stdin closes, so a plugin never outlives the Dexo
//! that started it, even one that died without stopping it.

mod families;

use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Write};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, PoisonError};

use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectionFactory, DbValue, QueryEvent, QueryId, QueryStream, RowBatch, Session};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{
    self as protocol, Cursor, InitializeParams, InitializeResult, OnSession, QueryExecute,
    QueryPage, Request, Response, RpcError, SessionOpenParams, SessionOpenResult, code, method,
};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use serde::Serialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use serde_json::Value;

/// Serves `factories` on stdin and stdout, and returns when stdin reaches its end.
/// `version` is the plugin's own, said in the handshake.
pub fn serve(factories: Vec<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>, version: &str) {
    let runtime = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_multi_thread()
        .enable_all()
        .build()
        .expect("a tokio runtime for the plugin");
    runtime.block_on(serve_io(
        BufReader::new(std::io::stdin()),
        std::io::stdout(),
        factories,
        version.to_string(),
    ));
}

/// Serves `factories` on a thread of this process, and returns the host's ends of the
/// pipes: what the plugin writes, to read, and what it reads, to write. For tests.
pub fn serve_in_process(
    factories: Vec<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>,
) -> std::io::Result<(std::io::PipeReader, std::io::PipeWriter)> {
    let (host_reader, plugin_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
    let (plugin_reader, host_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let runtime = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_multi_thread()
            .enable_all()
            .build()
            .expect("a tokio runtime for the plugin");
        runtime.block_on(serve_io(
            BufReader::new(plugin_reader),
            plugin_writer,
            factories,
            "0.0.0".into(),
        ));
    });
    Ok((host_reader, host_writer))
}

/// Answers every request in a task of its own, so a cancel is heard while a query runs
/// and one session's page never waits for another's.
pub async fn serve_io(
    reader: impl BufRead + Send + 'static,
    writer: impl Write + Send + 'static,
    factories: Vec<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>,
    version: String,
) {
    let (lines_tx, mut lines) = tokio::sync::mpsc::channel::<Vec<u8>>(64);
    std::thread::spawn(move || read_lines(reader, lines_tx));
    let (out, responses) = std::sync::mpsc::channel::<Response>();
    std::thread::spawn(move || write_lines(writer, responses));
    let server = Arc::new(Server {
        factories,
        version,
        sessions: Mutex::default(),
        cursors: Mutex::default(),
        next: AtomicU64::new(1),
        out,
    });
    while let Some(line) = lines.recv().await {
        let server = server.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let response = server.handle(&line).await;
            let _ = server.out.send(response);
        });
    }
    server.close_all().await;
}

/// The host's lines until its end, or until one too long, which ends the conversation.
fn read_lines(mut reader: impl BufRead, lines: tokio::sync::mpsc::Sender<Vec<u8>>) {
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    while let Ok(true) = protocol::read_line(&mut reader, &mut line) {
        if !line.is_empty() && lines.blocking_send(std::mem::take(&mut line)).is_err() {
            return;
        }
    }
}

fn write_lines(mut writer: impl Write, responses: std::sync::mpsc::Receiver<Response>) {
    for response in responses {
        if protocol::write_line(&mut writer, &response).is_err() {
            return;
        }
    }
}

/// A result being read, and the events read past the last page.
struct Open {
    stream: QueryStream,
    pending: VecDeque<QueryEvent>,
}

type Sessions = HashMap<String, Arc<Box<dyn Session>>>;
type Cursors = HashMap<String, (String, Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<Open>>)>;

pub(crate) struct Server {
    factories: Vec<Arc<dyn ConnectionFactory>>,
    version: String,
    sessions: Mutex<Sessions>,
    /// By cursor: the session it belongs to and the result.
    cursors: Mutex<Cursors>,
    next: AtomicU64,
    out: std::sync::mpsc::Sender<Response>,
}

impl Server {
    async fn handle(&self, line: &[u8]) -> Response {
        let request: Request = match serde_json::from_slice(line) {
            Ok(request) => request,
            Err(error) => {
                return Response::err(0, RpcError::new(code::PARSE_ERROR, error.to_string()));
            }
        };
        let id = request.id;
        match self.dispatch(request).await {
            Ok(result) => Response::ok(id, result),
            Err(error) => Response::err(id, error),
        }
    }

    async fn dispatch(&self, request: Request) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
        let params = request.params;
        match request.method.as_str() {
            method::INITIALIZE => reply(self.initialize(parse(params)?)?),
            method::SESSION_OPEN => reply(self.open(parse(params)?).await?),
            method::SESSION_CLOSE => {
                let call: OnSession<()> = parse(params)?;
                reply(self.close(&call.session).await?)
            }
            method::QUERY_EXECUTE => reply(self.execute(parse(params)?).await?),
            method::QUERY_FETCH => {
                let call: Cursor = parse(params)?;
                reply(self.fetch(&call.cursor).await?)
            }
            method::QUERY_CLOSE => {
                let call: Cursor = parse(params)?;
                lock(&self.cursors).remove(&call.cursor);
                reply(())
            }
            method::QUERY_CANCEL => {
                let call: OnSession<uuid::Uuid> = parse(params)?;
                let session = self.session(&call.session)?;
                session
                    .cancel(QueryId(call.args))
                    .await
                    .map_err(|error| RpcError::driver(&error))?;
                reply(())
            }
            other => families::dispatch(self, other, params).await,
        }
    }

    fn initialize(&self, params: InitializeParams) -> Result<InitializeResult, RpcError> {
        if !params.protocols.contains(&protocol::PROTOCOL) {
            return Err(RpcError::new(
                code::INVALID_REQUEST,
                format!(
                    "this plugin speaks protocol {}, and Dexo {} offered {:?}",
                    protocol::PROTOCOL,
                    params.dexo_version,
                    params.protocols
                ),
            ));
        }
        Ok(InitializeResult {
            protocol: protocol::PROTOCOL,
            driver_version: self.version.clone(),
            drivers: self.factories.iter().map(|f| f.driver_name()).collect(),
        })
    }

    async fn open(&self, params: SessionOpenParams) -> Result<SessionOpenResult, RpcError> {
        let factory = self
            .factories
            .iter()
            .find(|factory| factory.driver_name() == params.driver)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                RpcError::new(
                    code::INVALID_PARAMS,
                    format!("this plugin has no driver '{}'", params.driver),
                )
            })?;
        let session = factory
            .connect(params.into_request())
            .await
            .map_err(|error| RpcError::driver(&error))?;
        let id = format!("s{}", self.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed));
        let opened = SessionOpenResult {
            session: id.clone(),
            capabilities: session.capabilities().to_vec(),
            families: families::of(&*session),
            server_session_id: session.server_session_id(),
            transaction: session.transactions().map(|control| control.state()),
        };
        lock(&self.sessions).insert(id, Arc::new(session));
        Ok(opened)
    }

    pub(crate) fn session(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Arc<Box<dyn Session>>, RpcError> {
        lock(&self.sessions)
            .get(id)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| RpcError::new(code::UNKNOWN_SESSION, format!("no session '{id}'")))
    }

    async fn close(&self, id: &str) -> Result<(), RpcError> {
        lock(&self.cursors).retain(|_, (session, _)| session != id);
        let session = lock(&self.sessions)
            .remove(id)
            .ok_or_else(|| RpcError::new(code::UNKNOWN_SESSION, format!("no session '{id}'")))?;
        // A request still running holds the session: it is dropped when that one ends.
        match Arc::try_unwrap(session) {
            Ok(session) => session.close().await.map_err(|error| RpcError::driver(&error)),
            Err(_) => Ok(()),
        }
    }

    async fn close_all(&self) {
        lock(&self.cursors).clear();
        let sessions: Vec<_> = lock(&self.sessions).drain().map(|(_, session)| session).collect();
        for session in sessions {
            if let Ok(session) = Arc::try_unwrap(session) {
                let _ = session.close().await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn execute(&self, call: OnSession<QueryExecute>) -> Result<QueryPage, RpcError> {
        let session = self.session(&call.session)?;
        let stream = session
            .execute(call.args.into_request())
            .await
            .map_err(|error| RpcError::driver(&error))?;
        let mut open = Open {
            stream,
            pending: VecDeque::new(),
        };
        let mut page = next_page(&mut open).await;
        if page.done {
            page.transaction = session.transactions().map(|control| control.state());
        } else {
            let cursor = format!("c{}", self.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed));
            lock(&self.cursors).insert(
                cursor.clone(),
                (call.session, Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(open))),
            );
            page.cursor = Some(cursor);
        }
        Ok(page)
    }

    async fn fetch(&self, cursor: &str) -> Result<QueryPage, RpcError> {
        let (session, open) = lock(&self.cursors).get(cursor).cloned().ok_or_else(|| {
            RpcError::new(code::UNKNOWN_CURSOR, format!("no cursor '{cursor}'"))
        })?;
        let mut page = next_page(&mut *open.lock().await).await;
        if page.done {
            lock(&self.cursors).remove(cursor);
            page.transaction = self
                .session(&session)
                .ok()
                .and_then(|session| session.transactions().map(|control| control.state()));
        } else {
            page.cursor = Some(cursor.to_string());
        }
        Ok(page)
    }
}

fn lock<T>(mutex: &Mutex<T>) -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, T> {
    mutex.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
}

pub(crate) fn parse<T: DeserializeOwned>(params: Value) -> Result<T, RpcError> {
    serde_json::from_value(params)
        .map_err(|error| RpcError::new(code::INVALID_PARAMS, error.to_string()))
}

pub(crate) fn reply(result: impl Serialize) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    serde_json::to_value(result).map_err(|error| RpcError::new(code::INTERNAL, error.to_string()))
}

/// Events up to a page's worth -- [`protocol::PAGE_ROWS`] rows or about
/// [`protocol::PAGE_BYTES`] of values -- a batch larger than that split across pages.
/// The stream is read only here, so the driver works only as fast as the host reads.
async fn next_page(open: &mut Open) -> QueryPage {
    let mut events = Vec::new();
    let (mut rows, mut bytes) = (0usize, 0usize);
    let page = |events, done, error| QueryPage {
        cursor: None,
        events,
        done,
        error,
        transaction: None,
    };
    loop {
        if rows >= protocol::PAGE_ROWS || bytes >= protocol::PAGE_BYTES {
            return page(events, false, None);
        }
        let event = match open.pending.pop_front() {
            Some(event) => event,
            None => match open.stream.next().await {
                Some(Ok(event)) => event,
                Some(Err(error)) => return page(events, true, Some(error)),
                None => return page(events, true, None),
            },
        };
        let QueryEvent::Rows(batch) = event else {
            events.push(event);
            continue;
        };
        let room = protocol::PAGE_ROWS - rows;
        let mut taken = Vec::new();
        let mut rest = batch.rows.into_iter();
        for row in rest.by_ref() {
            bytes += row.iter().map(weight).sum::<usize>();
            taken.push(row);
            if taken.len() == room || bytes >= protocol::PAGE_BYTES {
                break;
            }
        }
        rows += taken.len();
        let rest: Vec<_> = rest.collect();
        if !rest.is_empty() {
            open.pending
                .push_front(QueryEvent::Rows(RowBatch { rows: rest }));
        }
        events.push(QueryEvent::Rows(RowBatch { rows: taken }));
    }
}

/// About how many bytes a value takes on the wire.
fn weight(value: &DbValue) -> usize {
    match value {
        DbValue::Null | DbValue::Bool(_) => 4,
        DbValue::I64(_) | DbValue::U64(_) => 20,
        DbValue::Decimal(text) | DbValue::Text(text) | DbValue::Json(text) => text.len() + 16,
        DbValue::Bytes(bytes) => bytes.len() * 4 / 3 + 16,
        DbValue::Native {
            type_name,
            bytes,
            text,
        } => type_name.len() + bytes.len() * 4 / 3 + text.len() + 32,
    }
}
```

```rust
use dexo_driver_api::{Capability, Session};

        .capabilities()
        .iter()
        .any(|state| state.capability == Capability::Cancel && state.available)

}

}
```

```rust
//! The families beyond queries: each maps a method onto the driver trait it names.

use dexo_driver_api::{
    Capability, CatalogListOptions, DataRequest, DbValue, DriverError, ExplainRequest, Mutation,
    ObjectId, QualifiedName, RemoteValueRef, Session, TransactionMode,
};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{Bytes, Family, OnSession, RpcError, TxResult, code, method};
use serde_json::Value;

use crate::{Server, parse, reply};

/// The families `session` answers.
pub(crate) fn of(session: &dyn Session) -> Vec<Family> {
    let mut families = Vec::new();
    if session.catalog().is_some() {
        families.push(Family::Catalog);
    }
    if session.data().is_some() {
        families.push(Family::Data);
    }
    if session.bulk().is_some() {
        families.push(Family::Bulk);
    }
    if session.explain().is_some() {
        families.push(Family::Explain);
    }
    if session.transactions().is_some() {
        families.push(Family::Transactions);
    }
    if session
        .capabilities()
        .iter()
        .any(|state| state.capability == Capability::Cancel && state.available)
    {
        families.push(Family::Cancel);
    }
    families
}

pub(crate) async fn dispatch(server: &Server, method: &str, params: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let family = method.split('/').next().unwrap_or_default();
    if !matches!(family, "catalog" | "data" | "bulk" | "explain" | "tx") {
        return Err(not_found(method));
    }
    let call: OnSession<Value> = parse(params)?;
    let session = server.session(&call.session)?;
    let session: &dyn Session = &**session;
    match family {
        "catalog" => catalog(session, method, call.args).await,
        "data" => data(session, method, call.args).await,
        "bulk" => bulk(session, call.args).await,
        "explain" => explain(session, call.args).await,
        _ => transactions(session, method, call.args).await,
    }
}

fn not_found(method: &str) -> RpcError {
    RpcError::new(code::METHOD_NOT_FOUND, format!("no method '{method}'"))
}

fn missing(family: &str) -> RpcError {
    RpcError::new(
        code::METHOD_NOT_FOUND,
        format!("this session has no {family} methods"),
    )
}

fn driver(error: DriverError) -> RpcError {
    RpcError::driver(&error)
}

async fn catalog(session: &dyn Session, name: &str, args: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let reader = session.catalog().ok_or_else(|| missing("catalog"))?;
    match name {
        method::CATALOG_LIST_CHILDREN => {
            let (parent, options): (Option<ObjectId>, CatalogListOptions) = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.list_children(parent.as_ref(), &options).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_OBJECT => {
            let id: ObjectId = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.object(&id).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_DDL => {
            let id: ObjectId = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.ddl(&id).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_DEPENDENCIES => {
            let id: ObjectId = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.dependencies(&id).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_DEPENDENTS => {
            let id: ObjectId = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.dependents(&id).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_FOREIGN_KEYS => {
            let table: QualifiedName = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.foreign_keys(&table).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_RELATIONS_NAMED => {
            let (schema, name): (Option<String>, String) = parse(args)?;
            reply(reader.relations_named(schema.as_deref(), &name).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::CATALOG_DATABASES => reply(reader.databases().await.map_err(driver)?),
        other => Err(not_found(other)),
    }
}

async fn data(session: &dyn Session, name: &str, args: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let mutator = session.data().ok_or_else(|| missing("data"))?;
    match name {
        method::DATA_FETCH => {
            let request: DataRequest = parse(args)?;
            reply(mutator.fetch(request).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::DATA_FETCH_VALUE => {
            let (value, offset, limit): (RemoteValueRef, u64, u32) = parse(args)?;
            reply(Bytes(mutator.fetch_value(&value, offset, limit).await.map_err(driver)?))
        }
        method::DATA_APPLY => {
            let mutations: Vec<Mutation> = parse(args)?;
            reply(mutator.apply(&mutations).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::DATA_TABLE_COLUMNS => {
            let table: QualifiedName = parse(args)?;
            reply(mutator.table_columns(&table).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        method::DATA_ESTIMATE_ROWS => {
            let table: QualifiedName = parse(args)?;
            reply(mutator.estimate_rows(&table).await.map_err(driver)?)
        }
        other => Err(not_found(other)),
    }
}

async fn bulk(session: &dyn Session, args: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let writer = session.bulk().ok_or_else(|| missing("bulk"))?;
    let (table, columns, rows): (QualifiedName, Vec<String>, Vec<Vec<DbValue>>) = parse(args)?;
    reply(writer.insert_batch(&table, &columns, &rows).await.map_err(driver)?)
}

async fn explain(session: &dyn Session, args: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let provider = session.explain().ok_or_else(|| missing("explain"))?;
    let request: ExplainRequest = parse(args)?;
    reply(provider.explain(request).await.map_err(driver)?)
}

/// Every transaction method answers the state it left, failed or not: the host's
/// `state()` is synchronous and reads what the last answer said.
async fn transactions(session: &dyn Session, name: &str, args: Value) -> Result<Value, RpcError> {
    let control = session.transactions().ok_or_else(|| missing("transactions"))?;
    let result = match name {
        method::TX_BEGIN => {
            let mode: TransactionMode = parse(args)?;
            control.begin(mode).await
        }
        method::TX_COMMIT => control.commit().await,
        method::TX_ROLLBACK => control.rollback().await,
        method::TX_SAVEPOINT => control.savepoint(&parse::<String>(args)?).await,
        method::TX_ROLLBACK_TO => control.rollback_to(&parse::<String>(args)?).await,
        method::TX_RELEASE_SAVEPOINT => control.release_savepoint(&parse::<String>(args)?).await,
        other => return Err(not_found(other)),
    };
    reply(TxResult {
        state: control.state(),
        notice: control.take_notice(),
        error: result.err(),
    })
}
```

```rust
//! The plugin host: one process shared, started on demand, checked by its handshake,
//! stopped when idle, not restarted forever. Plugins here are threads with pipes.
use std::io::BufReader;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use dexo_app::plugin::host::{PluginHost, Spawned, Spawner};
use dexo_driver_sqlite::SqliteFactory;
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{Request, Response, read_line, write_line};
use serde_json::{Value, json};

fn sqlite() -> Spawner {
    Box::new(|| {
        let (reader, writer) = dexo_plugin_sdk::serve_in_process(vec![Arc::new(SqliteFactory)])?;
        Ok(Spawned {
            reader: Box::new(reader),
            writer: Box::new(writer),
            stderr: None,
            child: None,
        })
    })
}

/// A plugin that answers the handshake with `reply`; it then hangs up when `then_exit`,
/// else waits for the host to.
fn fake(reply: Value, then_exit: bool) -> Spawner {
    Box::new(move || {
        let (host_reader, mut plugin_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
        let (plugin_reader, host_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
        let reply = reply.clone();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut reader = BufReader::new(plugin_reader);
            let mut line = Vec::new();
            if !read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap_or(false) {
                return;
            }
            let request: Request = serde_json::from_slice(&line).unwrap();
            let _ = write_line(&mut plugin_writer, &Response::ok(request.id, reply));
            if then_exit {
                return;
            }
            while read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap_or(false) {}
        });
        Ok(Spawned {
            reader: Box::new(host_reader),
            writer: Box::new(host_writer),
            stderr: None,
            child: None,
        })
    })
}

/// A plugin that reads and never answers.
fn silent() -> Spawner {
    Box::new(|| {
        let (host_reader, plugin_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
        let (plugin_reader, host_writer) = std::io::pipe()?;
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let _keep = plugin_writer;
            let mut reader = BufReader::new(plugin_reader);
            let mut line = Vec::new();
            while read_line(&mut reader, &mut line).unwrap_or(false) {}
        });
        Ok(Spawned {
            reader: Box::new(host_reader),
            writer: Box::new(host_writer),
            stderr: None,
            child: None,
        })
    })
}

fn handshake(protocol: u32, drivers: &[&str]) -> Value {
    json!({"protocol": protocol, "driver_version": "1.0.0", "drivers": drivers})
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_process_is_started_once_and_shared() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("SQLite", vec!["sqlite".into()], sqlite());
    let first = host.live().await.unwrap();
    let second = host.live().await.unwrap();
    assert!(Arc::ptr_eq(&first, &second));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_handshake_with_another_protocol_is_refused() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("Fake", vec!["fake".into()], fake(handshake(2, &["fake"]), false));
    let error = host.live().await.err().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        error.to_string(),
        "the Fake plugin is broken: it speaks protocol 2, and this Dexo speaks 1"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_handshake_missing_a_driver_is_refused() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("Fake", vec!["fake".into()], fake(handshake(1, &["other"]), false));
    let error = host.live().await.err().unwrap();
    assert!(
        error.to_string().contains("does not provide the fake driver"),
        "{error}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_silent_plugin_fails_its_handshake_after_ten_seconds() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("Fake", vec!["fake".into()], silent());
    let error = host.live().await.err().unwrap();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("within 10 seconds"), "{error}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_plugin_that_keeps_crashing_is_restarted_three_times_a_minute_at_most() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("Fake", vec!["fake".into()], fake(handshake(1, &["fake"]), true));
    for _ in 0..4 {
        let live = host.live().await.unwrap();
        while live.rpc.closed().is_none() {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5)).await;
        }
    }
    let error = host.live().await.err().unwrap();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("stopped 3 times in a minute"), "{error}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_idle_plugin_stops_and_starts_again_on_demand() {
    let host = Arc::new(
        PluginHost::new("SQLite", vec!["sqlite".into()], sqlite())
            .with_idle(Duration::from_millis(50)),
    );
    let live = host.live().await.unwrap();
    host.session_opened();
    host.session_closed();
    let started = Instant::now();
    while live.rpc.closed().is_none() {
        assert!(started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(5), "never stopped");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
    }
    // Stops on purpose are no crashes: the budget is untouched.
    for _ in 0..4 {
        host.live().await.unwrap();
        host.stop("again");
    }
    assert!(host.live().await.is_ok());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_stopped_plugin_says_why() {
    let host = PluginHost::new("SQLite", vec!["sqlite".into()], sqlite());
    let live = host.live().await.unwrap();
    host.stop("it was removed");
    let error = host
        .ask::<Value>(&live, "catalog/databases", json!({}), None)
        .await
        .err()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(error.to_string(), "the SQLite plugin stopped: it was removed");
}
```
