- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `src/plugin/rpc.rs`**

```rust
//! The host's end of the plugin protocol: requests out, replies matched back to them by
//! id. Two threads of its own carry the lines, so no async runtime has to outlive the
//! plugin -- the CLI's runtimes come and go.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Read, Write};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use dexo_plugin_protocol::{self as protocol, Request, Response, RpcError, code};
use serde::Serialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::sync::oneshot;

use super::lock;

/// Why the conversation ended.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Closed {
    pub why: String,
    /// The plugin broke the protocol, rather than going away.
    pub violation: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CallError {
    /// The plugin answered with an error.
    Remote(RpcError),
    /// The conversation is over.
    Closed(Closed),
    TimedOut,
}

type Reply = Result<Value, CallError>;

/// What the reading thread and the client share.
struct Shared {
    pending: Mutex<HashMap<u64, oneshot::Sender<Reply>>>,
    closed: Mutex<Option<Closed>>,
}

impl Shared {
    /// Ends the conversation: every call waiting fails, and so does every later one.
    /// The first reason given stays.
    fn shut(&self, closed: Closed) {
        let mut slot = lock(&self.closed);
        let closed = slot.get_or_insert(closed).clone();
        for (_, waiting) in lock(&self.pending).drain() {
            let _ = waiting.send(Err(CallError::Closed(closed.clone())));
        }
    }
}

pub struct RpcClient {
    next: AtomicU64,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    out: Mutex<Option<std::sync::mpsc::Sender<Vec<u8>>>>,
}

impl RpcClient {
    pub fn start(
        reader: impl Read + Send + 'static,
        writer: impl Write + Send + 'static,
    ) -> Arc<Self> {
        let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
            pending: Mutex::default(),
            closed: Mutex::default(),
        });
        let (out, lines) = std::sync::mpsc::channel::<Vec<u8>>();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut writer = writer;
            for line in lines {
                if writer.write_all(&line).and_then(|()| writer.flush()).is_err() {
                    return;
                }
            }
        });
        let reading = shared.clone();
        std::thread::spawn(move || read_replies(BufReader::new(reader), &reading));
        Arc::new(Self {
            next: AtomicU64::new(1),
            shared,
            out: Mutex::new(Some(out)),
        })
    }

    pub async fn call<R: DeserializeOwned>(
        &self,
        method: &str,
        params: impl Serialize,
        timeout: Option<Duration>,
    ) -> Result<R, CallError> {
        let id = self.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let line = encode(id, method, params)?;
        let (sender, receiver) = oneshot::channel();
        {
            // Under the closed lock: a shutdown cannot slip in between the check and
            // the insert and leave this call waiting for good.
            let closed = lock(&self.shared.closed);
            if let Some(closed) = closed.as_ref() {
                return Err(CallError::Closed(closed.clone()));
            }
            lock(&self.shared.pending).insert(id, sender);
        }
        if !self.send(line) {
            self.close(Closed {
                why: "it exited".into(),
                violation: false,
            });
        }
        let reply = match timeout {
            None => receiver.await,
            Some(limit) => match tokio::time::timeout(limit, receiver).await {
                Ok(reply) => reply,
                Err(_) => {
                    lock(&self.shared.pending).remove(&id);
                    return Err(CallError::TimedOut);
                }
            },
        };
        let value = reply.map_err(|_| {
            CallError::Closed(Closed {
                why: "it exited".into(),
                violation: false,
            })
        })??;
        serde_json::from_value(value).map_err(|error| {
            CallError::Remote(RpcError::new(
                code::INTERNAL,
                format!("an answer this Dexo cannot read: {error}"),
            ))
        })
    }

    /// Sends a request whose answer nobody waits for: what a dropped result or session
    /// sends on its way out, outside any runtime.
    pub fn notify(&self, method: &str, params: impl Serialize) {
        let id = self.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        if let Ok(line) = encode(id, method, params) {
            self.send(line);
        }
    }

    /// Ends the conversation. Waiting calls fail with `closed`, and the plugin's stdin
    /// closes, which a plugin takes as the time to exit.
    pub fn close(&self, closed: Closed) {
        self.shared.shut(closed);
        lock(&self.out).take();
    }

    pub fn closed(&self) -> Option<Closed> {
        lock(&self.shared.closed).clone()
    }

    fn send(&self, line: Vec<u8>) -> bool {
        lock(&self.out)
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|out| out.send(line).is_ok())
    }
}

fn read_replies(mut reader: impl BufRead, shared: &Shared) {
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    let closed = loop {
        match protocol::read_line(&mut reader, &mut line) {
            Ok(false) => {
                break Closed {
                    why: "it exited".into(),
                    violation: false,
                };
            }
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidData => {
                break Closed {
                    why: format!("it broke the protocol: {error}"),
                    violation: true,
                };
            }
            Err(error) => {
                break Closed {
                    why: format!("its output could not be read: {error}"),
                    violation: false,
                };
            }
            Ok(true) if line.is_empty() => {}
            Ok(true) => match serde_json::from_slice::<Response>(&line) {
                Ok(response) => {
                    let waiting = lock(&shared.pending).remove(&response.id);
                    if let Some(waiting) = waiting {
                        let _ = waiting.send(match response.error {
                            Some(error) => Err(CallError::Remote(error)),
                            None => Ok(response.result.unwrap_or(Value::Null)),
                        });
                    }
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    break Closed {
                        why: "it wrote something that is not the plugin protocol".into(),
                        violation: true,
                    };
                }
            },
        }
    };
    shared.shut(closed);
}

fn encode(id: u64, method: &str, params: impl Serialize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CallError> {
    let internal = |error: serde_json::Error| {
        CallError::Remote(RpcError::new(code::INTERNAL, error.to_string()))
    };
    let request = Request::new(id, method, params).map_err(internal)?;
    let mut line = serde_json::to_vec(&request).map_err(internal)?;
    line.push(b'\n');
    Ok(line)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `src/plugin/host.rs`**

```rust
//! A plugin's process: started on its first connection and shared by all its sessions,
//! stopped a minute after the last one closes, and restarted after a crash -- not more
//! than three times a minute.

use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Read, Write};
use std::process::Child;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use dexo_driver_api::{DriverError, DriverErrorCategory};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{InitializeParams, InitializeResult, PROTOCOL, method};
use serde::Serialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;

use super::lock;
use super::rpc::{CallError, Closed, RpcClient};

pub const HANDSHAKE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
pub const IDLE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
pub const CANCEL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
const RESTARTS: usize = 3;
const LOG_LINES: usize = 20;

/// A started plugin: its pipes, its log, and its process when it is one.
pub struct Spawned {
    pub reader: Box<dyn Read + Send>,
    pub writer: Box<dyn Write + Send>,
    pub stderr: Option<Box<dyn Read + Send>>,
    pub child: Option<Child>,
}

/// How a plugin is started: a process in Dexo, a thread in tests.
pub type Spawner = Box<dyn Fn() -> std::io::Result<Spawned> + Send + Sync>;

/// One run of a plugin.
pub struct Live {
    pub rpc: Arc<RpcClient>,
    log: Arc<Mutex<VecDeque<String>>>,
    /// Hangs up when stderr ends: what a crash said is read before it is reported.
    log_done: Mutex<Option<std::sync::mpsc::Receiver<()>>>,
    child: Mutex<Option<Child>>,
    /// Stopped on purpose -- idle, removed, Dexo leaving -- rather than crashed.
    stopped: AtomicBool,
}

impl Live {
    fn start(spawned: Spawned) -> Self {
        let log: Arc<Mutex<VecDeque<String>>> = Arc::default();
        let (done, log_done) = std::sync::mpsc::channel::<()>();
        if let Some(stderr) = spawned.stderr {
            let log = log.clone();
            std::thread::spawn(move || {
                let _done = done;
                for line in BufReader::new(stderr).lines() {
                    let Ok(line) = line else { return };
                    let mut log = lock(&log);
                    if log.len() == LOG_LINES {
                        log.pop_front();
                    }
                    log.push_back(line);
                }
            });
        }
        Self {
            rpc: RpcClient::start(spawned.reader, spawned.writer),
            log,
            log_done: Mutex::new(Some(log_done)),
            child: Mutex::new(spawned.child),
            stopped: AtomicBool::new(false),
        }
    }

    /// The plugin's last line of log, once its stderr had a moment to end.
    fn last_log(&self) -> Option<String> {
        if let Some(done) = lock(&self.log_done).take() {
            let _ = done.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(500));
        }
        lock(&self.log).back().cloned()
    }

    /// Stops the process, when there is one, and waits for it.
    fn reap(&self) {
        if let Some(mut child) = lock(&self.child).take() {
            crate::process::stop_group(&mut child);
        }
    }
}

pub struct PluginHost {
    name: String,
    drivers: Vec<String>,
    spawn: Spawner,
    idle: Duration,
    starting: tokio::sync::Mutex<()>,
    state: Mutex<HostState>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct HostState {
    live: Option<Arc<Live>>,
    sessions: usize,
    /// Bumped by every open and close: an idle timer acts only if nothing happened since.
    ticket: u64,
    crashes: VecDeque<Instant>,
}

impl PluginHost {
    /// `name` is what messages call the plugin; `drivers` what its handshake must list.
    pub fn new(name: impl Into<String>, drivers: Vec<String>, spawn: Spawner) -> Self {
        Self {
            name: name.into(),
            drivers,
            spawn,
            idle: IDLE,
            starting: tokio::sync::Mutex::new(()),
            state: Mutex::default(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_idle(mut self, idle: Duration) -> Self {
        self.idle = idle;
        self
    }

    pub fn name(&self) -> &str {
        &self.name
    }

    /// The running process, started -- and its handshake checked -- when there is none.
    pub async fn live(&self) -> Result<Arc<Live>, DriverError> {
        let _one_at_a_time = self.starting.lock().await;
        let previous = lock(&self.state).live.clone();
        if let Some(previous) = previous {
            if previous.rpc.closed().is_none() {
                return Ok(previous);
            }
            if !previous.stopped.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
                let now = Instant::now();
                let mut state = lock(&self.state);
                state
                    .crashes
                    .retain(|at| now.duration_since(*at) < Duration::from_secs(60));
                if state.crashes.len() >= RESTARTS {
                    return Err(DriverError::new(
                        DriverErrorCategory::Network,
                        format!(
                            "the {} plugin stopped {RESTARTS} times in a minute: try again in a minute",
                            self.name
                        ),
                    ));
                }
                state.crashes.push_back(now);
            }
            previous.reap();
            lock(&self.state).live = None;
        }
        let spawned = (self.spawn)().map_err(|error| {
            DriverError::new(
                DriverErrorCategory::Configuration,
                format!("the {} plugin did not start: {error}", self.name),
            )
        })?;
        let live = Arc::new(Live::start(spawned));
        if let Err(why) = self.handshake(&live).await {
            self.stop_live(&live, &why);
            return Err(DriverError::new(
                DriverErrorCategory::Internal,
                format!("the {} plugin is broken: {why}", self.name),
            ));
        }
        lock(&self.state).live = Some(live.clone());
        Ok(live)
    }

    async fn handshake(&self, live: &Live) -> Result<(), String> {
        let offered = InitializeParams {
            dexo_version: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION").into(),
            protocols: vec![PROTOCOL],
        };
        let reply: InitializeResult =
            match live.rpc.call(method::INITIALIZE, offered, Some(HANDSHAKE)).await {
                Ok(reply) => reply,
                Err(CallError::TimedOut) => {
                    return Err("it did not answer the handshake within 10 seconds".into());
                }
                Err(CallError::Remote(error)) => return Err(error.message),
                Err(CallError::Closed(closed)) => {
                    return Err(live.last_log().unwrap_or(closed.why));
                }
            };
        if reply.protocol != PROTOCOL {
            return Err(format!(
                "it speaks protocol {}, and this Dexo speaks {PROTOCOL}",
                reply.protocol
            ));
        }
        if let Some(missing) = self
            .drivers
            .iter()
            .find(|driver| !reply.drivers.contains(driver))
        {
            return Err(format!(
                "it does not provide the {missing} driver its manifest declares"
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A call on `live`, its failures in the words a session's error uses.
    pub async fn ask<R: DeserializeOwned>(
        &self,
        live: &Live,
        method: &str,
        params: impl Serialize,
        timeout: Option<Duration>,
    ) -> Result<R, DriverError> {
        match live.rpc.call(method, params, timeout).await {
            Ok(reply) => Ok(reply),
            Err(CallError::Remote(error)) => Err(error.into_driver_error()),
            Err(CallError::Closed(closed)) => Err(self.failure(live, &closed)),
            Err(CallError::TimedOut) => Err(DriverError::new(
                DriverErrorCategory::Timeout,
                format!("the {} plugin did not answer in time", self.name),
            )),
        }
    }

    /// "the DuckDB plugin stopped: <why>" -- for a crash, the last thing it logged.
    fn failure(&self, live: &Live, closed: &Closed) -> DriverError {
        let why = if live.stopped.load(Ordering::SeqCst) || closed.violation {
            closed.why.clone()
        } else {
            live.last_log().unwrap_or_else(|| closed.why.clone())
        };
        let category = if closed.violation {
            DriverErrorCategory::Internal
        } else {
            DriverErrorCategory::Network
        };
        DriverError::new(category, format!("the {} plugin stopped: {why}", self.name))
    }

    fn stop_live(&self, live: &Live, why: &str) {
        live.stopped.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        live.rpc.close(Closed {
            why: why.into(),
            violation: false,
        });
        live.reap();
    }

    /// Kills `live` now -- a driver that did not answer a cancel in time -- failing its
    /// sessions with `why`. The next connection starts it again.
    pub fn kill(&self, live: &Live, why: &str) {
        self.stop_live(live, why);
    }

    /// Stops the process for good -- removed, disabled, updated -- failing its sessions
    /// with `why`.
    pub fn stop(&self, why: &str) {
        let live = lock(&self.state).live.take();
        if let Some(live) = live {
            self.stop_live(&live, why);
        }
    }

    pub fn session_opened(&self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        state.sessions += 1;
        state.ticket += 1;
    }

    /// With no session left, the process stops after the idle time unless one opens
    /// meanwhile.
    pub fn session_closed(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        let ticket = {
            let mut state = lock(&self.state);
            state.sessions = state.sessions.saturating_sub(1);
            state.ticket += 1;
            if state.sessions > 0 {
                return;
            }
            state.ticket
        };
        let host = self.clone();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            std::thread::sleep(host.idle);
            let idle = {
                let state = lock(&host.state);
                state.sessions == 0 && state.ticket == ticket
            };
            if idle {
                host.stop("it was idle");
            }
        });
    }
}
```

```rust
//! What the proxy adds over the wire: a result dropped midway frees its session, and
//! the transaction state follows what the plugin reports.
use std::sync::Arc;

use dexo_app::plugin::host::{PluginHost, Spawned};
use dexo_app::plugin::proxy::PluginFactory;
use dexo_driver_api::{
    ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DbValue, DriverDescriptor, QueryEvent, QueryRequest,
    Session, TransactionMode, TransactionState,
};
use dexo_driver_sqlite::SqliteFactory;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use secrecy::SecretString;

async fn connect(dir: &tempfile::TempDir) -> Box<dyn Session> {
    let host = PluginHost::new(
        "SQLite",
        vec!["sqlite".into()],
        Box::new(|| {
            let (reader, writer) =
                dexo_plugin_sdk::serve_in_process(vec![Arc::new(SqliteFactory)])?;
            Ok(Spawned {
                reader: Box::new(reader),
                writer: Box::new(writer),
                stderr: None,
                child: None,
            })
        }),
    );
    PluginFactory::new(DriverDescriptor::sqlite(), Arc::new(host))
        .connect(ConnectRequest::new(
            dir.path().join("a.db").to_string_lossy(),
            None,
            String::new(),
            SecretString::from(String::new()),
            false,
        ))
        .await
        .unwrap()
}

async fn rows(session: &dyn Session, sql: &str) -> Vec<Vec<DbValue>> {
    let mut stream = session.execute(QueryRequest::read(sql, 0)).await.unwrap();
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
        if let QueryEvent::Rows(batch) = event.unwrap() {
            rows.extend(batch.rows);
        }
    }
    rows
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn dropping_a_result_midway_frees_the_session() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let session = connect(&dir).await;
    let sql = "WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i + 1 FROM n WHERE i < 1200) SELECT i FROM n";
    let mut stream = session.execute(QueryRequest::read(sql, 0)).await.unwrap();
    stream.next().await.unwrap().unwrap();
    drop(stream);
    assert_eq!(rows(&*session, "SELECT 7").await, vec![vec![DbValue::I64(7)]]);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_transaction_state_follows_the_plugin() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let session = connect(&dir).await;
    assert!(session.catalog().is_some() && session.data().is_some());
    assert!(session.bulk().is_some() && session.explain().is_some());
    let control = session.transactions().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(control.state(), TransactionState::Idle);
    control.begin(TransactionMode::ReadWrite).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(control.state(), TransactionState::Active);
    control.rollback().await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(control.state(), TransactionState::Idle);
    session.close().await.unwrap();
}
```

```rust
//! A driver plugin as Dexo's other drivers are: a `ConnectionFactory` whose sessions
//! forward every call to the plugin's process.

use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use dexo_driver_api::{
    BulkWriter, CapabilityState, CatalogList, CatalogListOptions, CatalogObject, CatalogReader,
    ColumnKeyInfo, ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DataMutator, DataPage, DataRequest, DbValue,
    DriverDescriptor, DriverError, DriverErrorCategory, ExplainPlan, ExplainProvider,
    ExplainRequest, ForeignKeyRef, Mutation, ObjectDdl, ObjectId, QualifiedName, QueryEvent,
    QueryId, QueryRequest, QueryStream, RemoteValueRef, Session, TransactionControl,
    TransactionMode, TransactionState,
};
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{
    Bytes, Cursor, Family, OnSession, QueryExecute, QueryPage, SessionOpenParams,
    SessionOpenResult, TxResult, method,
};
use serde::Serialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;

use super::host::{CANCEL, Live, PluginHost};
use super::lock;

pub struct PluginFactory {
    descriptor: DriverDescriptor,
    host: Arc<PluginHost>,
}

impl PluginFactory {
    pub fn new(descriptor: DriverDescriptor, host: Arc<PluginHost>) -> Self {
        Self { descriptor, host }
    }

    pub fn host(&self) -> &Arc<PluginHost> {
        &self.host
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl ConnectionFactory for PluginFactory {
    fn descriptor(&self) -> DriverDescriptor {
        self.descriptor.clone()
    }

    async fn connect(&self, request: ConnectRequest) -> Result<Box<dyn Session>, DriverError> {
        let live = self.host.live().await?;
        let opened: SessionOpenResult = self
            .host
            .ask(
                &live,
                method::SESSION_OPEN,
                SessionOpenParams::new(&self.descriptor.id, &request),
                None,
            )
            .await?;
        self.host.session_opened();
        Ok(Box::new(ProxySession {
            host: self.host.clone(),
            live,
            id: opened.session,
            capabilities: opened.capabilities,
            families: opened.families,
            server_session_id: opened.server_session_id,
            transaction: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Tx {
                state: opened.transaction.unwrap_or(TransactionState::Unknown),
                notice: None,
            })),
            closed: AtomicBool::new(false),
        }))
    }
}

/// The transaction state and notice the plugin last reported.
struct Tx {
    state: TransactionState,
    notice: Option<String>,
}

pub struct ProxySession {
    host: Arc<PluginHost>,
    live: Arc<Live>,
    id: String,
    capabilities: Vec<CapabilityState>,
    families: Vec<Family>,
    server_session_id: Option<String>,
    transaction: Arc<Mutex<Tx>>,
    closed: AtomicBool,
}

impl ProxySession {
    async fn call<R: DeserializeOwned>(
        &self,
        method: &str,
        args: impl Serialize + Send,
    ) -> Result<R, DriverError> {
        let params = OnSession {
            session: self.id.clone(),
            args,
        };
        self.host.ask(&self.live, method, params, None).await
    }

    fn has(&self, family: Family) -> bool {
        self.families.contains(&family)
    }

    async fn tx(&self, method: &str, args: impl Serialize + Send) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        let result: TxResult = self.call(method, args).await?;
        {
            let mut tx = lock(&self.transaction);
            tx.state = result.state;
            tx.notice = result.notice;
        }
        result.error.map_or(Ok(()), Err)
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl Session for ProxySession {
    fn capabilities(&self) -> &[CapabilityState] {
        &self.capabilities
    }

    async fn execute(&self, request: QueryRequest) -> Result<QueryStream, DriverError> {
        let first: QueryPage = self
            .call(method::QUERY_EXECUTE, QueryExecute::new(&request))
            .await?;
        let mut pages = Pages {
            host: self.host.clone(),
            live: self.live.clone(),
            session: self.id.clone(),
            query: request.id,
            events: VecDeque::new(),
            cursor: None,
            error: None,
            transaction: self.transaction.clone(),
        };
        pages.take(first);
        Ok(Box::pin(pages.into_stream()))
    }

    /// A driver that does not answer a cancel in time is killed: a query nobody can stop
    /// would otherwise hold the session for good.
    async fn cancel(&self, query: QueryId) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        let params = OnSession {
            session: self.id.clone(),
            args: query.0,
        };
        match self
            .host
            .ask::<()>(&self.live, method::QUERY_CANCEL, params, Some(CANCEL))
            .await
        {
            Err(error) if error.category() == DriverErrorCategory::Timeout => {
                let why = "it did not answer a cancel within 5 seconds";
                self.host.kill(&self.live, why);
                Err(DriverError::new(
                    DriverErrorCategory::Network,
                    format!("the {} plugin stopped: {why}", self.host.name()),
                ))
            }
            other => other,
        }
    }

    async fn close(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.closed.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        let result = self.call::<()>(method::SESSION_CLOSE, ()).await;
        self.host.session_closed();
        result
    }

    fn transactions(&self) -> Option<&dyn TransactionControl> {
        self.has(Family::Transactions)
            .then_some(self as &dyn TransactionControl)
    }

    fn catalog(&self) -> Option<&dyn CatalogReader> {
        self.has(Family::Catalog).then_some(self as &dyn CatalogReader)
    }

    fn data(&self) -> Option<&dyn DataMutator> {
        self.has(Family::Data).then_some(self as &dyn DataMutator)
    }

    fn bulk(&self) -> Option<&dyn BulkWriter> {
        self.has(Family::Bulk).then_some(self as &dyn BulkWriter)
    }

    fn explain(&self) -> Option<&dyn ExplainProvider> {
        self.has(Family::Explain).then_some(self as &dyn ExplainProvider)
    }

    fn server_session_id(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.server_session_id.clone()
    }
}

impl Drop for ProxySession {
    /// A session dropped without `close` still frees its place in the plugin.
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if !self.closed.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            self.live.rpc.notify(
                method::SESSION_CLOSE,
                OnSession {
                    session: self.id.clone(),
                    args: (),
                },
            );
            self.host.session_closed();
        }
    }
}

/// A result read page by page as it is polled: the next page is asked for only once the
/// last is used up. Dropped before its end, it cancels the query and frees the cursor.
struct Pages {
    host: Arc<PluginHost>,
    live: Arc<Live>,
    session: String,
    query: QueryId,
    events: VecDeque<QueryEvent>,
    cursor: Option<String>,
    error: Option<DriverError>,
    transaction: Arc<Mutex<Tx>>,
}

impl Pages {
    fn take(&mut self, page: QueryPage) {
        self.events.extend(page.events);
        self.cursor = if page.done { None } else { page.cursor };
        self.error = page.error;
        if let Some(state) = page.transaction {
            lock(&self.transaction).state = state;
        }
    }

    fn into_stream(
        self,
    ) -> impl futures_util::Stream<Item = Result<QueryEvent, DriverError>> + Send {
        futures_util::stream::unfold(self, |mut pages| async move {
            loop {
                if let Some(event) = pages.events.pop_front() {
                    return Some((Ok(event), pages));
                }
                if let Some(error) = pages.error.take() {
                    return Some((Err(error), pages));
                }
                let cursor = pages.cursor.clone()?;
                let next = pages
                    .host
                    .ask::<QueryPage>(&pages.live, method::QUERY_FETCH, Cursor { cursor }, None)
                    .await;
                match next {
                    Ok(page) => pages.take(page),
                    Err(error) => {
                        pages.cursor = None;
                        return Some((Err(error), pages));
                    }
                }
            }
        })
    }
}

impl Drop for Pages {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if let Some(cursor) = self.cursor.take() {
            self.live.rpc.notify(
                method::QUERY_CANCEL,
                OnSession {
                    session: self.session.clone(),
                    args: self.query.0,
                },
            );
            self.live
                .rpc
                .notify(method::QUERY_CLOSE, Cursor { cursor });
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl CatalogReader for ProxySession {
    async fn list_children(
        &self,
        parent: Option<&ObjectId>,
        options: &CatalogListOptions,
    ) -> Result<CatalogList, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_LIST_CHILDREN, (parent, options)).await
    }

    async fn object(&self, id: &ObjectId) -> Result<Option<CatalogObject>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_OBJECT, id).await
    }

    async fn ddl(&self, id: &ObjectId) -> Result<ObjectDdl, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_DDL, id).await
    }

    async fn dependencies(&self, id: &ObjectId) -> Result<Vec<ObjectId>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_DEPENDENCIES, id).await
    }

    async fn dependents(&self, id: &ObjectId) -> Result<Vec<ObjectId>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_DEPENDENTS, id).await
    }

    async fn foreign_keys(&self, table: &QualifiedName) -> Result<Vec<ForeignKeyRef>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_FOREIGN_KEYS, table).await
    }

    async fn relations_named(
        &self,
        schema: Option<&str>,
        name: &str,
    ) -> Result<Option<Vec<CatalogObject>>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_RELATIONS_NAMED, (schema, name)).await
    }

    async fn databases(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::CATALOG_DATABASES, ()).await
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl DataMutator for ProxySession {
    async fn fetch(&self, request: DataRequest) -> Result<DataPage, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::DATA_FETCH, request).await
    }

    async fn fetch_value(
        &self,
        value: &RemoteValueRef,
        offset: u64,
        limit: u32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, DriverError> {
        let bytes: Bytes = self
            .call(method::DATA_FETCH_VALUE, (value, offset, limit))
            .await?;
        Ok(bytes.0)
    }

    async fn apply(&self, mutations: &[Mutation]) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.call(method::DATA_APPLY, mutations).await
    }

    async fn table_columns(&self, target: &QualifiedName) -> Result<Vec<ColumnKeyInfo>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::DATA_TABLE_COLUMNS, target).await
    }

    async fn estimate_rows(&self, target: &QualifiedName) -> Result<Option<u64>, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::DATA_ESTIMATE_ROWS, target).await
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl BulkWriter for ProxySession {
    async fn insert_batch(
        &self,
        table: &QualifiedName,
        columns: &[String],
        rows: &[Vec<DbValue>],
    ) -> Result<u64, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::BULK_INSERT_BATCH, (table, columns, rows)).await
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl ExplainProvider for ProxySession {
    async fn explain(&self, request: ExplainRequest) -> Result<ExplainPlan, DriverError> {
        self.call(method::EXPLAIN, request).await
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl TransactionControl for ProxySession {
    async fn begin(&self, mode: TransactionMode) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_BEGIN, mode).await
    }

    async fn commit(&self) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_COMMIT, ()).await
    }

    async fn rollback(&self) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_ROLLBACK, ()).await
    }

    async fn savepoint(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_SAVEPOINT, name).await
    }

    async fn rollback_to(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_ROLLBACK_TO, name).await
    }

    async fn release_savepoint(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.tx(method::TX_RELEASE_SAVEPOINT, name).await
    }

    fn state(&self) -> TransactionState {
        lock(&self.transaction).state
    }

    fn take_notice(&self) -> Option<String> {
        lock(&self.transaction).notice.take()
    }
}
```

```rust
//! The SQLite driver's contract again, with the driver behind the plugin protocol: the
//! SDK serves it and Dexo's proxy talks to it, one plugin for every session, as in Dexo.
//! What passes here, a plugin driver does as the built-in one does.
mod suite;

use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};

use dexo_app::plugin::host::{PluginHost, Spawned};
use dexo_app::plugin::proxy::PluginFactory;
use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DriverDescriptor, Session};
use dexo_driver_sqlite::SqliteFactory;

fn plugin() -> &'static PluginFactory {
    static PLUGIN: OnceLock<PluginFactory> = OnceLock::new();
    PLUGIN.get_or_init(|| {
        let host = PluginHost::new(
            "SQLite",
            vec!["sqlite".into()],
            Box::new(|| {
                let (reader, writer) =
                    dexo_plugin_sdk::serve_in_process(vec![Arc::new(SqliteFactory)])?;
                Ok(Spawned {
                    reader: Box::new(reader),
                    writer: Box::new(writer),
                    stderr: None,
                    child: None,
                })
            }),
        );
        PluginFactory::new(DriverDescriptor::sqlite(), Arc::new(host))
    })
}

async fn connect(request: ConnectRequest) -> Box<dyn Session> {
    plugin().connect(request).await.expect("connect")
}
```

### Task 8: Plugin processes, and a test plugin that misbehaves

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dexo-app/src/plugin/host.rs` (append `process_spawner`, `plugin_env`, `dev_override`)
- Create: `crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/Cargo.toml`, `src/main.rs`, `tests/process.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 6–7): `PluginHost`, `Spawned`, `Spawner`, `PluginFactory`, `crate::process::{detach, register, stop_group}`.
- Produces:
  - `plugin::host::process_spawner(executable: PathBuf) -> Spawner` — the process gets its own process group, piped stdio and the minimal environment, and is registered for `stop_all`.
  - `plugin::host::plugin_env(vars: impl Iterator<Item = (OsString, OsString)>) -> Vec<(OsString, OsString)>`.
  - `plugin::host::dev_override(plugin_id: &str) -> Option<PathBuf>` reads `DEXO_DRIVER_<ID>`.
  - The testbin binary `dexo-plugin-testbin` provides driver `testbin`; its behaviour depends on the name it runs under (`crash`, `ignore-cancel`, `bad-protocol`, `print-env`, anything else is plain).

`crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/src/main.rs`:

```rust
//! A driver plugin for Dexo's tests: SQLite served by the SDK as driver `testbin`,
//! misbehaving as the name it runs under says, so the host's handling of each failure
//! is tested against a real process:
//! - `crash` exits when a statement mentions `crash`, saying so on stderr;
//! - `ignore-cancel` never answers a cancel;
//! - `bad-protocol` answers the handshake with protocol 99;
//! - `print-env` writes the names of its environment next to itself, then serves.

use std::io::BufReader;
use std::sync::Arc;

use dexo_driver_api::{
    CapabilityState, ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DriverDescriptor, DriverError, QueryId,
    QueryRequest, QueryStream, Session,
};
use dexo_driver_sqlite::SqliteFactory;
use dexo_plugin_protocol::{Request, Response, read_line, write_line};

fn main() {
    let exe = std::env::current_exe().unwrap_or_default();
    let mode = exe
        .file_stem()
        .map(|stem| stem.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    match mode.as_str() {
        "bad-protocol" => bad_protocol(),
        "print-env" => {
            let names: Vec<String> = std::env::vars_os()
                .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
                .collect();
            let _ = std::fs::write(exe.with_extension("env"), names.join("\n"));
            serve(mode);
        }
        _ => serve(mode),
    }
}

fn serve(mode: String) {
    dexo_plugin_sdk::serve(vec![Arc::new(Testing { mode })], "0.1.0");
}

/// Answers the handshake with a protocol no Dexo speaks, then waits to be stopped.
fn bad_protocol() {
    let mut stdin = BufReader::new(std::io::stdin());
    let mut line = Vec::new();
    if let Ok(true) = read_line(&mut stdin, &mut line)
        && let Ok(request) = serde_json::from_slice::<Request>(&line)
    {
        let reply = serde_json::json!({"protocol": 99, "driver_version": "0.1.0", "drivers": ["testbin"]});
        let _ = write_line(&mut std::io::stdout(), &Response::ok(request.id, reply));
    }
    while let Ok(true) = read_line(&mut stdin, &mut line) {}
}

struct Testing {
    mode: String,
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl ConnectionFactory for Testing {
    fn descriptor(&self) -> DriverDescriptor {
        DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver("testbin", "Testbin", true, "testbin", true)
    }

    async fn connect(&self, request: ConnectRequest) -> Result<Box<dyn Session>, DriverError> {
        let inner = SqliteFactory.connect(request).await?;
        Ok(Box::new(Misbehaving {
            inner,
            mode: self.mode.clone(),
        }))
    }
}

struct Misbehaving {
    inner: Box<dyn Session>,
    mode: String,
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl Session for Misbehaving {
    fn capabilities(&self) -> &[CapabilityState] {
        self.inner.capabilities()
    }

    async fn execute(&self, request: QueryRequest) -> Result<QueryStream, DriverError> {
        if self.mode == "crash" && request.sql.contains("crash") {
            eprintln!("crashing on purpose");
            std::process::exit(3);
        }
        self.inner.execute(request).await
    }

    async fn cancel(&self, query: QueryId) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        if self.mode == "ignore-cancel" {
            std::future::pending::<()>().await;
        }
        self.inner.cancel(query).await
    }

    async fn close(self: Box<Self>) -> Result<(), DriverError> {
        self.inner.close().await
    }
}
```

`crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/tests/process.rs`:

```rust
//! The host against a real plugin process: the testbin, copied under the name of the
//! failure it plays.
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use dexo_app::plugin::host::{PluginHost, process_spawner};
use dexo_app::plugin::proxy::PluginFactory;
use dexo_driver_api::{
    ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DbValue, DriverDescriptor, DriverError, QueryEvent,
    QueryRequest, Session,
};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use secrecy::SecretString;

/// The testbin in `dir`, named `mode`.
fn testbin(dir: &Path, mode: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let exe = dir.join(format!("{mode}{}", std::env::consts::EXE_SUFFIX));
    std::fs::copy(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_dexo-plugin-testbin"), &exe).unwrap();
    exe
}

fn plugin(dir: &Path, mode: &str) -> PluginFactory {
    let host = PluginHost::new(
        "Testbin",
        vec!["testbin".into()],
        process_spawner(testbin(dir, mode)),
    );
    PluginFactory::new(
        DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver("testbin", "Testbin", true, "testbin", true),
        Arc::new(host),
    )
}

async fn open(plugin: &PluginFactory, dir: &Path) -> Result<Box<dyn Session>, DriverError> {
    plugin
        .connect(ConnectRequest::new(
            dir.join("t.db").to_string_lossy(),
            None,
            String::new(),
            SecretString::from(String::new()),
            false,
        ))
        .await
}

async fn run(session: &dyn Session, sql: &str) -> Result<Vec<Vec<DbValue>>, DriverError> {
    let mut stream = session.execute(QueryRequest::read(sql, 0)).await?;
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
        if let QueryEvent::Rows(batch) = event? {
            rows.extend(batch.rows);
        }
    }
    Ok(rows)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_plugin_process_answers_queries() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugin = plugin(dir.path(), "plain");
    let session = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(run(&*session, "SELECT 1").await.unwrap(), vec![vec![DbValue::I64(1)]]);
    session.close().await.unwrap();
}

/// Review focus 1.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_killed_plugin_fails_open_sessions_and_restarts() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugin = plugin(dir.path(), "crash");
    let session = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
    let crashed = run(&*session, "SELECT 'crash'").await.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(crashed.to_string(), "the Testbin plugin stopped: crashing on purpose");
    let after = run(&*session, "SELECT 1").await.unwrap_err();
    assert!(after.to_string().starts_with("the Testbin plugin stopped"), "{after}");
    let again = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(run(&*again, "SELECT 1").await.unwrap(), vec![vec![DbValue::I64(1)]]);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_restart_budget_holds() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugin = plugin(dir.path(), "crash");
    for _ in 0..4 {
        let session = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
        let _ = run(&*session, "SELECT 'crash'").await;
    }
    let refused = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.err().unwrap();
    assert!(refused.to_string().contains("stopped 3 times in a minute"), "{refused}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_plugin_that_ignores_a_cancel_is_killed() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugin = plugin(dir.path(), "ignore-cancel");
    let session = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
    let request = QueryRequest::read(
        "WITH RECURSIVE n(i) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT i + 1 FROM n) SELECT count(*) FROM n",
        0,
    );
    let id = request.id;
    let started = Instant::now();
    let (ran, cancelled) = tokio::join!(
        async {
            let mut stream = session.execute(request).await?;
            while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
                event?;
            }
            Ok::<_, DriverError>(())
        },
        async {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
            session.cancel(id).await
        },
    );
    assert_eq!(
        cancelled.unwrap_err().to_string(),
        "the Testbin plugin stopped: it did not answer a cancel within 5 seconds"
    );
    assert!(ran.unwrap_err().to_string().contains("did not answer a cancel"));
    assert!(started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(15));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_handshake_with_another_protocol_is_refused() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let refused = open(&plugin(dir.path(), "bad-protocol"), dir.path())
        .await
        .err()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(refused.to_string().contains("it speaks protocol 99"), "{refused}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_plugin_gets_a_minimal_environment() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugin = plugin(dir.path(), "print-env");
    let session = open(&plugin, dir.path()).await.unwrap();
    let names = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("print-env.env")).unwrap();
    let allowed = [
        "PATH", "HOME", "USERPROFILE", "TMPDIR", "TMP", "TEMP", "LANG", "LANGUAGE", "SYSTEMROOT",
    ];
    for name in names.lines() {
        let upper = name.to_ascii_uppercase();
        assert!(
            allowed.contains(&upper.as_str()) || upper.starts_with("LC_"),
            "{name} reached the plugin"
        );
    }
    assert!(names.lines().any(|name| name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("PATH")));
    session.close().await.unwrap();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** — append to `crates/dexo-app/src/plugin/host.rs`:

```rust
/// Starts `executable` as a plugin: a process group of its own, stopped with Dexo, and
/// an environment with nothing of Dexo's in it -- the connection's secrets go in the
/// `session/open` message, never in a variable.
pub fn process_spawner(executable: std::path::PathBuf) -> Spawner {
    Box::new(move || {
        let mut command = std::process::Command::new(&executable);
        crate::process::detach(&mut command);
        command
            .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped())
            .env_clear()
            .envs(plugin_env(std::env::vars_os()));
        let mut child = command.spawn()?;
        crate::process::register(child.id());
        let missing = || std::io::Error::other("the plugin's pipes were not opened");
        Ok(Spawned {
            reader: Box::new(child.stdout.take().ok_or_else(missing)?),
            writer: Box::new(child.stdin.take().ok_or_else(missing)?),
            stderr: child
                .stderr
                .take()
                .map(|stderr| Box::new(stderr) as Box<dyn Read + Send>),
            child: Some(child),
        })
    })
}

/// The variables a plugin gets: where programs and home are, the temporary directory,
/// the locale, and on Windows `SystemRoot`, without which programs fail to start.
pub fn plugin_env(
    vars: impl Iterator<Item = (std::ffi::OsString, std::ffi::OsString)>,
) -> Vec<(std::ffi::OsString, std::ffi::OsString)> {
    vars.filter(|(name, _)| {
        let upper = name.to_string_lossy().to_ascii_uppercase();
        matches!(
            upper.as_str(),
            "PATH" | "HOME" | "USERPROFILE" | "TMPDIR" | "TMP" | "TEMP" | "LANG" | "LANGUAGE"
                | "SYSTEMROOT"
        ) || upper.starts_with("LC_")
    })
    .collect()
}

/// `DEXO_DRIVER_<ID>` runs a local build in place of plugin `<ID>`, for developing one.
pub fn dev_override(plugin: &str) -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let name = format!("DEXO_DRIVER_{}", plugin.to_ascii_uppercase().replace('-', "_"));
    std::env::var_os(name)
        .filter(|path| !path.is_empty())
        .map(std::path::PathBuf::from)
}
```

`crate::process::detach` sets stdin to null; the explicit `stdin(Stdio::piped())` after it replaces that. `detach`, `register` and `stop_group` are `pub(crate)`, so no visibility change is needed.

```rust
//! DuckDB's contract through the plugin executable Dexo runs: one process for every
//! session, as in Dexo, so two sessions on one file share its database.
mod suite;

use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};

use dexo_app::plugin::host::{PluginHost, process_spawner};
use dexo_app::plugin::proxy::PluginFactory;
use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectRequest, ConnectionFactory, DriverDescriptor, Session};

fn plugin() -> &'static PluginFactory {
    static PLUGIN: OnceLock<PluginFactory> = OnceLock::new();
    PLUGIN.get_or_init(|| {
        let host = PluginHost::new(
            "DuckDB",
            vec!["duckdb".into()],
            process_spawner(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_dexo-driver-duckdb").into()),
        );
        PluginFactory::new(DriverDescriptor::duckdb(), Arc::new(host))
    })
}

async fn connect(request: ConnectRequest) -> Box<dyn Session> {
    plugin().connect(request).await.expect("connect")
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;

use dexo_driver_api::DriverDescriptor;

use crate::driver_registry::DriverRegistry;

/// Drivers Dexo knows of that a local build provides through `DEXO_DRIVER_<ID>`:
/// developing the DuckDB plugin needs no package.
pub fn register_dev_overrides(registry: &DriverRegistry) {
    for descriptor in DriverDescriptor::known() {
        let Some(plugin) = descriptor.plugin.clone() else {
            continue;
        };
        if registry.has(&descriptor.id) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(executable) = host::dev_override(&plugin) {
            let host = host::PluginHost::new(
                descriptor.display_name.to_string(),
                vec![descriptor.id.to_string()],
                host::process_spawner(executable),
            );
            registry.insert(Arc::new(proxy::PluginFactory::new(descriptor, Arc::new(host))));
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::unpack;

    const FILE: tar::EntryType = tar::EntryType::Regular;

    /// A `.tar.gz` of `entries`, their names written raw as a hostile package's would be:
    /// `tar`'s own `set_path` refuses `..` and absolute paths.
    fn package(entries: &[(&str, tar::EntryType)]) -> Vec<u8> {
        let mut builder = tar::Builder::new(flate2::write::GzEncoder::new(
            Vec::new(),
            flate2::Compression::fast(),
        ));
        for (name, kind) in entries {
            let mut header = tar::Header::new_gnu();
            header.as_old_mut().name[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name.as_bytes());
            header.set_entry_type(*kind);
            header.set_size(1);
            header.set_mode(0o644);
            if matches!(kind, tar::EntryType::Symlink | tar::EntryType::Link) {
                header.set_link_name("dexo-plugin.toml").unwrap();
                header.set_size(0);
            }
            header.set_cksum();
            let data: &[u8] = if header.size().unwrap() == 1 { b"x" } else { b"" };
            builder.append(&header, data).unwrap();
        }
        builder.into_inner().unwrap().finish().unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_plugin_unpacks_with_a_runnable_executable() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let bytes = package(&[
            ("dexo-plugin.toml", FILE),
            ("README.md", FILE),
            ("LICENSE", FILE),
            ("bin/tool", FILE),
            ("themes/a.toml", FILE),
        ]);
        unpack(&bytes[..], dir.path()).unwrap();
        assert!(dir.path().join("themes/a.toml").is_file());
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
            let mode = std::fs::metadata(dir.path().join("bin/tool"))
                .unwrap()
                .permissions()
                .mode();
            assert_eq!(mode & 0o111, 0o111);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn what_could_escape_or_surprise_is_refused() {
        for (name, kind) in [
            ("../evil", FILE),
            ("/etc/evil", FILE),
            ("bin/../../evil", FILE),
            ("C:evil", FILE),
            ("src/lib.rs", FILE),
            ("bin/sub/tool", FILE),
            ("themes/a.txt", FILE),
            ("bin/link", tar::EntryType::Symlink),
            ("bin/hard", tar::EntryType::Link),
        ] {
            let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
            let bytes = package(&[(name, kind)]);
            assert!(unpack(&bytes[..], dir.path()).is_err(), "{name} was let through");
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! `dexo-plugin.toml`: what a plugin is and what it provides, read without running any
//! of it -- so the connection form can list a driver that is not installed.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use dexo_driver_api::DriverDescriptor;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialEq, PartialOrd, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Kind {
    Driver,
    Theme,
}

impl Kind {
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Kind::Driver => "driver",
            Kind::Theme => "theme",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Manifest {
    pub schema: u32,
    pub id: String,
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub version: String,
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub authors: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub license: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub homepage: String,
    pub min_dexo: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub driver: Option<DriverSection>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub theme: Option<ThemeSection>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct DriverSection {
    pub protocol: u32,
    pub drivers: Vec<String>,
    pub display_name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dialect: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub file: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub url_schemes: Vec<String>,
    pub executable: String,
}

/// What a driver plugin provides, as the index lists it. Unknown keys are allowed here:
/// a newer index may say more than this Dexo reads.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DriverInfo {
    pub protocol: u32,
    pub drivers: Vec<String>,
    pub display_name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dialect: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub file: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub url_schemes: Vec<String>,
}

impl DriverSection {
    pub fn info(&self) -> DriverInfo {
        DriverInfo {
            protocol: self.protocol,
            drivers: self.drivers.clone(),
            display_name: self.display_name.clone(),
            dialect: self.dialect.clone(),
            file: self.file,
            url_schemes: self.url_schemes.clone(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct ThemeSection {
    pub themes: Vec<String>,
}

/// Reads and checks a manifest. Strict: an unknown key is an error, so a typo does not
/// pass for a setting.
pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Result<Manifest, String> {
    let manifest: Manifest =
        toml::from_str(text).map_err(|error| format!("dexo-plugin.toml: {}", error.message()))?;
    manifest.validate()?;
    Ok(manifest)
}

/// The manifest of the plugin in `dir`, when it reads.
pub fn read(dir: &Path) -> Option<Manifest> {
    parse(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("dexo-plugin.toml")).ok()?).ok()
}

/// `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$`: an id names a folder, so nothing in it can climb out.
pub fn valid_id(id: &str) -> bool {
    let bytes = id.as_bytes();
    (2..=32).contains(&bytes.len())
        && (bytes[0].is_ascii_lowercase() || bytes[0].is_ascii_digit())
        && bytes
            .iter()
            .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || *byte == b'-')
}

impl Manifest {
    fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let fail = |what: String| Err(format!("dexo-plugin.toml: {what}"));
        if self.schema != 1 {
            return fail(format!("schema {} is newer than this Dexo reads", self.schema));
        }
        if !valid_id(&self.id) {
            return fail(format!(
                "'{}' is not a plugin id: 2 to 32 lowercase letters, digits and '-'",
                self.id
            ));
        }
        for (field, version) in [("version", &self.version), ("min_dexo", &self.min_dexo)] {
            if !crate::update::is_version(version) {
                return fail(format!("{field} '{version}' is not a version like 1.2.3"));
            }
        }
        match (self.kind, &self.driver, &self.theme) {
            (Kind::Driver, Some(driver), None) => {
                if driver.drivers.is_empty() || !driver.drivers.iter().all(|id| valid_id(id)) {
                    return fail("[driver] drivers must name at least one driver id".into());
                }
                let executable = &driver.executable;
                if !executable.starts_with("bin/")
                    || executable.matches('/').count() != 1
                    || !super::archive::safe_relative(executable)
                {
                    return fail("[driver] executable must be a file directly under bin/".into());
                }
                Ok(())
            }
            (Kind::Theme, None, Some(theme)) => {
                if theme.themes.is_empty()
                    || !theme
                        .themes
                        .iter()
                        .all(|path| path.starts_with("themes/") && path.ends_with(".toml"))
                {
                    return fail("[theme] themes must be files under themes/ ending in .toml".into());
                }
                Ok(())
            }
            (Kind::Driver, ..) => {
                fail("a driver plugin has a [driver] section and no [theme] section".into())
            }
            (Kind::Theme, ..) => {
                fail("a theme plugin has a [theme] section and no [driver] section".into())
            }
        }
    }

    /// The executable in the plugin's folder `dir`, `.exe` added on Windows.
    pub fn executable(&self, dir: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.driver.as_ref().map(|driver| {
            dir.join(format!("{}{}", driver.executable, std::env::consts::EXE_SUFFIX))
        })
    }

    /// The drivers this plugin brings, as Dexo lists them once it is installed.
    pub fn descriptors(&self) -> Vec<DriverDescriptor> {
        self.driver
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|driver| {
                driver.drivers.iter().map(|id| {
                    DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver(
                        id.clone(),
                        driver.display_name.clone(),
                        driver.file,
                        self.id.clone(),
                        true,
                    )
                })
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

/// What an installed plugin in `dir` provides: its driver ids, or its themes' names.
pub fn provides(manifest: &Manifest, dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    if let Some(driver) = &manifest.driver {
        return driver.drivers.clone();
    }
    let mut names: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("themes"))
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .filter_map(Result::ok)
        .map(|entry| entry.path())
        .filter(|path| path.extension().is_some_and(|extension| extension == "toml"))
        .filter_map(|path| Some(path.file_stem()?.to_string_lossy().into_owned()))
        .collect();
    names.sort();
    names
}
```

`crates/dexo-app/src/plugin/archive.rs` (the tests above go at its end):

```rust
//! Plugin packages, unpacked: nothing let out of the plugin's folder, and only the
//! files a plugin may ship let in.

use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Component, Path};

use super::manifest::{Kind, Manifest};

/// More than this unpacked is a compression bomb, not a plugin.
const MAX_UNPACKED: u64 = 1024 * 1024 * 1024;

/// Unpacks a `.tar.gz` into `into`. Refused: a symlink, a hard link, an absolute path, a
/// `..`, and any file a plugin does not ship.
pub fn unpack(package: impl Read, into: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let unreadable = |error: std::io::Error| format!("the package cannot be read: {error}");
    let mut archive = tar::Archive::new(flate2::read::GzDecoder::new(package));
    let mut total = 0u64;
    for entry in archive.entries().map_err(unreadable)? {
        let mut entry = entry.map_err(unreadable)?;
        let name = entry
            .path()
            .map_err(unreadable)?
            .to_string_lossy()
            .replace('\\', "/");
        let name = name.trim_start_matches("./").trim_end_matches('/').to_string();
        if name.is_empty() || name == "." {
            continue;
        }
        if !safe_relative(&name) {
            return Err(format!("the package holds '{name}', outside the plugin's folder"));
        }
        let kind = entry.header().entry_type();
        if kind.is_dir() {
            if !matches!(name.as_str(), "bin" | "themes") {
                return Err(format!("the package holds a folder '{name}', which a plugin does not ship"));
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(into.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
            continue;
        }
        if !kind.is_file() {
            return Err(format!("the package holds '{name}', which is not a plain file"));
        }
        if !allowed(&name) {
            return Err(format!("the package holds '{name}', which a plugin does not ship"));
        }
        total += entry.header().size().unwrap_or(0);
        if total > MAX_UNPACKED {
            return Err("the package unpacks to more than 1 GB".into());
        }
        let target = into.join(&name);
        if let Some(parent) = target.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        }
        let mut file = std::fs::File::create(&target)
            .map_err(|error| format!("{}: {error}", target.display()))?;
        std::io::copy(&mut entry, &mut file)
            .map_err(|error| format!("{}: {error}", target.display()))?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        if name.starts_with("bin/") {
            use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
            std::fs::set_permissions(&target, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755))
                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The files a plugin ships: its manifest, licence and readme, and a program directly
/// under `bin/` or themes directly under `themes/`.
pub(crate) fn allowed(name: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(name, "dexo-plugin.toml" | "LICENSE" | "README.md")
        || (name.starts_with("bin/") && name.matches('/').count() == 1)
        || (name.starts_with("themes/") && name.ends_with(".toml") && name.matches('/').count() == 1)
}

/// A relative path with no `..`, no root, no drive and no stream: nothing in it climbs
/// out of the folder it is joined to, on any system.
pub fn safe_relative(name: &str) -> bool {
    !name.is_empty()
        && !name.contains(':')
        && !name.contains('\\')
        && Path::new(name)
            .components()
            .all(|component| matches!(component, Component::Normal(_)))
}

/// What a package holds against its manifest: a driver's executable is there and it
/// ships no themes; a theme pack ships themes and no program.
pub fn check_kind(dir: &Path, manifest: &Manifest) -> Result<(), String> {
    match manifest.kind {
        Kind::Driver => {
            if dir.join("themes").exists() {
                return Err("a driver plugin ships no themes".into());
            }
            let executable = manifest.executable(dir).unwrap_or_default();
            if !executable.is_file() {
                let shown = executable.strip_prefix(dir).unwrap_or(&executable);
                return Err(format!("the package has no {}", shown.display()));
            }
        }
        Kind::Theme => {
            if dir.join("bin").exists() {
                return Err("a theme plugin ships no programs".into());
            }
            let themes = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("themes"))
                .map(|entries| {
                    entries
                        .filter_map(Result::ok)
                        .filter(|entry| entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "toml"))
                        .count()
                })
                .unwrap_or(0);
            if themes == 0 {
                return Err("the theme pack has no themes".into());
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/dexo-app/src/plugin/state.rs`, `src/plugin/install.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dexo-app/src/plugin/mod.rs` (`pub mod install; pub mod state;`, `plugins_dir`, `register`, `unregister`, `trust_text`)
- Create: `crates/dexo-test-support/src/plugins.rs`; modify `crates/dexo-test-support/Cargo.toml` and `src/lib.rs`
- Test: `crates/dexo-app/tests/plugin_install.rs`, `crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/tests/install.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 10): `manifest::{Manifest, Kind, parse, read, valid_id}`, `archive::{unpack, allowed, check_kind}`. Consumes (Tasks 6–8): `PluginHost`, `process_spawner`, `dev_override`, `PluginFactory`.
- Produces:
  - `plugin::plugins_dir(data_dir: &Path) -> PathBuf`.
  - `plugin::register(registry, data_dir, id)` and `plugin::unregister(registry, id, why)`.
  - `plugin::trust_text(&Manifest) -> String`.
  - `plugin::state`:
    - `Origin { Official, Local }`
    - `Installed { version, kind, enabled, origin, sha256: Option<String>, files: BTreeMap<String, String>, installed_at: u64 }`
    - `State { plugins: BTreeMap<String, Installed> }`
    - `load(plugins: &Path) -> State`
    - `save(plugins, &State) -> io::Result<()>`
    - `change<T>(plugins, FnOnce(&mut State) -> T) -> Result<T, AppError>`
    - `Lock::acquire(plugins, name) -> Result<Lock, AppError>`
    - `manifests(plugins) -> Vec<(String, Manifest)>`
  - `plugin::install`:
    - `Staged { pub manifest, pub sha256 }` with `path()`
    - `stage(data_dir, source: &Path) -> Result<Staged, AppError>`
    - `stage_bytes(data_dir, &[u8]) -> Result<Staged, AppError>`
    - `commit(data_dir, Staged, Origin, Option<&DriverRegistry>) -> Result<Manifest, AppError>`
    - `remove(data_dir, id, Option<&DriverRegistry>) -> Result<(), AppError>`
    - `set_enabled(data_dir, id, bool, Option<&DriverRegistry>) -> Result<(), AppError>`
    - `pub(crate) check_handshake(&Manifest, dir) -> Result<(), AppError>`
    - `pub(crate) hex(&[u8]) -> String`
  - `dexo_test_support::plugins::{THEME, manifest, theme_folder, driver_folder, pack}`.
- All of `install` is synchronous. A driver's handshake runs on a private current-thread runtime, so never call these functions from inside an async task: the TUI calls them on a blocking thread.

```rust
//! Plugin packages for tests, built on the spot: theme packs, driver plugins around a
//! test executable, and the `.tar.gz` of either.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

/// A theme, in the format Dexo reads.
pub const THEME: &str = "name = \"Ocean\"\nmode = \"dark\"\n[roles]\nfocus = \"#00aaff\"\n";

/// A manifest: a theme pack, or a driver plugin when `executable` names its program.
pub fn manifest(id: &str, version: &str, min_dexo: &str, executable: Option<&str>) -> String {
    let (kind, section) = match executable {
        Some(executable) => (
            "driver",
            format!(
                "[driver]\nprotocol = 1\ndrivers = [\"{id}\"]\ndisplay_name = \"Testbin\"\nfile = true\nexecutable = \"bin/{executable}\"\n"
            ),
        ),
        None => ("theme", "[theme]\nthemes = [\"themes/*.toml\"]\n".to_string()),
    };
    format!(
        "schema = 1\nid = \"{id}\"\nkind = \"{kind}\"\nversion = \"{version}\"\nname = \"{id}\"\nauthors = [\"Tests\"]\nmin_dexo = \"{min_dexo}\"\n\n{section}"
    )
}

/// A theme pack's folder in `dir`, with one theme, `ocean`.
pub fn theme_folder(dir: &Path, id: &str, version: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let root = dir.join(format!("{id}-{version}"));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("themes")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(root.join("dexo-plugin.toml"), manifest(id, version, "1.0.0", None)).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(root.join("themes/ocean.toml"), THEME).unwrap();
    root
}

/// A driver plugin's folder in `dir`: its program a copy of `executable`, named `name`
/// -- the test plugin picks how it behaves by the name it runs under.
pub fn driver_folder(dir: &Path, id: &str, version: &str, executable: &Path, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let root = dir.join(format!("{id}-{version}-{name}"));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("bin")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(
        root.join("dexo-plugin.toml"),
        manifest(id, version, "1.0.0", Some(name)),
    )
    .unwrap();
    std::fs::copy(
        executable,
        root.join("bin")
            .join(format!("{name}{}", std::env::consts::EXE_SUFFIX)),
    )
    .unwrap();
    root
}

/// `folder` as a plugin's `.tar.gz`.
pub fn pack(folder: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut builder = tar::Builder::new(flate2::write::GzEncoder::new(
        Vec::new(),
        flate2::Compression::fast(),
    ));
    for file in files(folder) {
        let name = file
            .strip_prefix(folder)
            .unwrap()
            .to_string_lossy()
            .replace('\\', "/");
        builder.append_path_with_name(&file, name).unwrap();
    }
    builder.into_inner().unwrap().finish().unwrap()
}

fn files(dir: &Path) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir).unwrap() {
        let path = entry.unwrap().path();
        if path.is_dir() {
            found.extend(files(&path));
        } else {
            found.push(path);
        }
    }
    found.sort();
    found
}
```

```rust
//! Installing from a file or a folder: put in place whole or not at all, recorded in
//! `state.json`, and guarded by a lock against a second Dexo.
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime};

use dexo_app::plugin::install;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::{self, Lock, Origin};
use dexo_test_support::plugins::{pack, theme_folder};

#[test]
fn a_theme_pack_installs_from_a_folder_and_from_a_package() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    let staged = install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(staged.manifest.id, "pack");
    install::commit(data.path(), staged, Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let plugins = data.path().join("plugins");
    assert!(plugins.join("pack/themes/ocean.toml").is_file());
    let installed = &state::load(&plugins).plugins["pack"];
    assert_eq!(
        (installed.version.as_str(), installed.origin, installed.enabled),
        ("1.0.0", Origin::Local, true)
    );
    assert!(installed.files.contains_key("themes/ocean.toml"));
    // The same pack as a package, a version on: an update in place, its checksum kept.
    let newer = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.1.0");
    let file = work.path().join("pack.tar.gz");
    std::fs::write(&file, pack(&newer)).unwrap();
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &file).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let installed = &state::load(&plugins).plugins["pack"];
    assert_eq!(installed.version, "1.1.0");
    assert!(installed.sha256.as_ref().is_some_and(|sha| sha.len() == 64));
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_dir(plugins.join(".staging")).unwrap().count(), 0);
}

#[test]
fn disabling_and_removing_change_the_state() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let plugins = data.path().join("plugins");
    install::set_enabled(data.path(), "pack", false, None).unwrap();
    assert!(!state::load(&plugins).plugins["pack"].enabled);
    install::remove(data.path(), "pack", None).unwrap();
    assert!(!plugins.join("pack").exists());
    assert!(state::load(&plugins).plugins.is_empty());
    let error = install::remove(data.path(), "pack", None).unwrap_err();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("no plugin 'pack' is installed"), "{error}");
}

#[test]
fn the_state_is_rebuilt_from_the_folders() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let plugins = data.path().join("plugins");
    std::fs::write(plugins.join("state.json"), "{ truncated").unwrap();
    let rebuilt = state::load(&plugins);
    assert_eq!(rebuilt.plugins["pack"].version, "1.0.0");
    assert!(rebuilt.plugins["pack"].enabled);
    std::fs::remove_dir_all(plugins.join("pack")).unwrap();
    assert!(state::load(&plugins).plugins.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn a_plugin_for_a_newer_dexo_is_refused() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    let manifest = folder.join("dexo-plugin.toml");
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&manifest).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&manifest, text.replace("min_dexo = \"1.0.0\"", "min_dexo = \"99.0.0\"")).unwrap();
    let error = install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None)
        .unwrap_err();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("needs Dexo 99.0.0 or newer"), "{error}");
    assert!(!data.path().join("plugins/pack").exists());
}

#[test]
fn a_held_lock_refuses_a_second_install_and_a_stale_one_is_taken_over() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let plugins = data.path().join("plugins");
    let held = Lock::acquire(&plugins, "pack").unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    let error = install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None)
        .unwrap_err();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("another Dexo is installing or removing plugins"), "{error}");
    std::mem::forget(held);
    let lock = plugins.join(".locks/pack.lock");
    let old = SystemTime::now() - Duration::from_secs(11 * 60);
    std::fs::File::options().write(true).open(&lock).unwrap().set_modified(old).unwrap();
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn a_package_with_a_file_a_plugin_does_not_ship_is_refused() {
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    std::fs::write(folder.join("install.sh"), "rm -rf ~").unwrap();
    let error = install::stage(data.path(), &folder).err().unwrap();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("install.sh"), "{error}");
}
```

`crates/dexo-plugin-testbin/tests/install.rs` (driver plugins; real processes):

```rust
//! Installing and removing a driver plugin: its handshake is checked before it goes in
//! place, and removing it ends its sessions cleanly. One test at a time: the plugin's
//! process is registered process-wide under its id.
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Mutex;

use dexo_app::DriverRegistry;
use dexo_app::plugin::install;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::{self, Origin};
use dexo_driver_api::{ConnectRequest, DbValue, QueryEvent, QueryRequest, Session};
use dexo_test_support::plugins::driver_folder;
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use secrecy::SecretString;

static ONE_AT_A_TIME: Mutex<()> = Mutex::new(());

fn testbin() -> &'static Path {
    Path::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_dexo-plugin-testbin"))
}

fn request(dir: &Path) -> ConnectRequest {
    ConnectRequest::new(
        dir.join("t.db").to_string_lossy(),
        None,
        String::new(),
        SecretString::from(String::new()),
        false,
    )
}

async fn run(session: &dyn Session, sql: &str) -> Result<Vec<Vec<DbValue>>, dexo_driver_api::DriverError> {
    let mut stream = session.execute(QueryRequest::read(sql, 0)).await?;
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
        if let QueryEvent::Rows(batch) = event? {
            rows.extend(batch.rows);
        }
    }
    Ok(rows)
}

/// Review focus 3.
#[test]
fn a_failed_update_keeps_the_previous_version() {
    let _one = ONE_AT_A_TIME.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let v1 = driver_folder(work.path(), "testbin", "1.0.0", testbin(), "plain");
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &v1).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let v2 = driver_folder(work.path(), "testbin", "1.1.0", testbin(), "bad-protocol");
    let error = install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &v2).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None)
        .unwrap_err();
    assert!(error.to_string().contains("protocol 99"), "{error}");
    let plugins = data.path().join("plugins");
    let manifest = std::fs::read_to_string(plugins.join("testbin/dexo-plugin.toml")).unwrap();
    assert!(manifest.contains("version = \"1.0.0\""), "{manifest}");
    assert_eq!(state::load(&plugins).plugins["testbin"].version, "1.0.0");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_dir(plugins.join(".staging")).unwrap().count(), 0);
}

/// Review focus 5.
#[test]
fn removing_a_plugin_ends_its_sessions_cleanly() {
    let _one = ONE_AT_A_TIME.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let folder = driver_folder(work.path(), "testbin", "1.0.0", testbin(), "plain");
    let registry = DriverRegistry::new();
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &folder).unwrap(), Origin::Local, Some(&registry))
        .unwrap();
    let runtime = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().unwrap();
    runtime.block_on(async {
        let session = registry
            .get("testbin")
            .unwrap()
            .connect(request(work.path()))
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(run(&*session, "SELECT 1").await.unwrap(), vec![vec![DbValue::I64(1)]]);
        install::remove(data.path(), "testbin", Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let error = run(&*session, "SELECT 1").await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error.to_string(), "the Testbin plugin stopped: it was removed");
    });
    let missing = registry.get("testbin").err().unwrap();
    assert!(
        missing
            .to_string()
            .contains("Testbin is a plugin: install it with `dexo plugin install testbin`"),
        "{missing}"
    );
    assert!(!data.path().join("plugins/testbin").exists());
}
```
