```rust
//! The Plugins screen: what is installed and what the index offers, the pick in full
//! with what can be done to it, the index's state said over the list.
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use dexo_app::plugin::manifest::Kind;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::{Installed, Origin};
use dexo_tui::model::Screen;
use dexo_tui::mouse::HitMap;
use dexo_tui::screens::plugins::{Busy, FromFile, PluginRow, PluginsSnapshot, StagedInfo};
use dexo_tui::{Action, Effect, Model, update};

fn paint(model: &mut Model, width: u16, height: u16) -> String {
    let mut terminal =
        ratatui::Terminal::new(ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(width, height)).unwrap();
    let mut hits = HitMap::default();
    terminal
        .draw(|frame| dexo_tui::render::render(frame, model, &mut hits))
        .unwrap();
    model.hits = hits;
    dexo_tui::render::render_to_string(model, width, height)
}

fn press(model: &mut Model, code: KeyCode) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let effects = update(model, Action::Key(KeyEvent::new(code, KeyModifiers::NONE)));
    paint(model, 120, 30);
    effects
}

fn installed(version: &str, enabled: bool, origin: Origin) -> Installed {
    Installed {
        version: version.into(),
        kind: Kind::Driver,
        enabled,
        origin,
        sha256: None,
        files: Default::default(),
        installed_at: 0,
    }
}

fn row(id: &str, name: &str, kind: Kind, installed: Option<Installed>, newest: Option<&str>) -> PluginRow {
    PluginRow {
        id: id.into(),
        name: name.into(),
        kind,
        description: format!("The {name} plugin"),
        authors: vec!["KingDasWinx".into()],
        license: "MIT".into(),
        installed: installed.map(|mut entry| {
            entry.kind = kind;
            entry
        }),
        newest: newest.map(Into::into),
        min_dexo: Some("1.5.0".into()),
        protocol: (kind == Kind::Driver).then_some(1),
        size: Some(27_839_411),
        provides: vec![id.into()],
        readme: vec![],
    }
}

fn rows() -> Vec<PluginRow> {
    vec![
        row("duckdb", "DuckDB", Kind::Driver, Some(installed("1.0.0", true, Origin::Official)), Some("1.1.0")),
        row("mine", "Mine", Kind::Theme, Some(installed("0.1.0", false, Origin::Local)), None),
        row("extra-themes", "Extra themes", Kind::Theme, None, Some("1.0.0")),
    ]
}

fn screen() -> Model {
    let mut model = Model::default();
    model.capabilities.unicode = true;
    let effects = update(&mut model, Action::GoToScreen(Screen::Plugins));
    assert!(
        effects
            .iter()
            .any(|effect| matches!(effect, Effect::LoadPlugins { refresh: false })),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    update(
        &mut model,
        Action::PluginsLoaded(PluginsSnapshot {
            rows: rows(),
            index_error: None,
            offline: None,
        }),
    );
    paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    model
}

#[test]
fn installed_plugins_list_with_their_state_and_actions() {
    let mut model = screen();
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("[Plugins 1]"), "the update count beside its name:\n{shown}");
    assert!(shown.contains("↑ DuckDB") && shown.contains("○ Mine"), "{shown}");
    assert!(shown.contains("local"), "{shown}");
    assert!(!shown.contains("Extra themes"), "available plugins wait in their view:\n{shown}");
    for button in ["[u Update to 1.1.0]", "[e Disable]", "[x Remove…]", "[r Refresh]", "[f Install from file…]"] {
        assert!(shown.contains(button), "{button}:\n{shown}");
    }
    assert!(shown.contains("official, signed") && shown.contains("26.5 MB"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(paint(&mut model, 80, 24));
}

#[test]
fn the_available_view_and_the_type_filter() {
    let mut model = screen();
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Tab);
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("+ Extra themes") && shown.contains("[i Install]"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(paint(&mut model, 80, 24));
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('t'));
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("Type: drivers") && shown.contains("No plugin matches"), "{shown}");
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Esc);
    assert!(paint(&mut model, 120, 30).contains("+ Extra themes"));
}

#[test]
fn under_80_columns_the_list_comes_alone() {
    let mut model = screen();
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 70, 24);
    assert!(shown.contains("DuckDB") && !shown.contains("[u Update"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(shown);
}

#[test]
fn the_index_state_is_said_over_the_list() {
    let mut model = screen();
    update(
        &mut model,
        Action::PluginsLoaded(PluginsSnapshot {
            rows: rows(),
            index_error: Some("the index signature does not match: nothing was installed".into()),
            offline: None,
        }),
    );
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("the index signature does not match: nothing was installed"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!("signature_mismatch", shown);
    update(
        &mut model,
        Action::PluginsLoaded(PluginsSnapshot {
            rows: rows(),
            index_error: None,
            offline: Some("offline: showing the index from 2026-10-05".into()),
        }),
    );
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("offline: showing the index from 2026-10-05"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!("offline", shown);
}

#[test]
fn a_download_shows_a_bar_with_bytes_and_percent() {
    let mut model = screen();
    model.plugins.busy = Some(Busy {
        id: "duckdb".into(),
        verb: "Updating",
        done: 13_919_706,
        total: Some(27_839_411),
    });
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("50%") && shown.contains("13.3 MB of 26.5 MB"), "{shown}");
    assert!(shown.contains("Esc cancels"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(shown);
}

#[test]
fn the_trust_prompt_asks_for_the_plugin_id() {
    let mut model = screen();
    model.plugins.from_file = Some(FromFile {
        staged: Some(StagedInfo {
            token: 1,
            id: "lake".into(),
            trust: "Lake 1.0.0 (driver, id lake) by someone is not from Dexo's signed index: nothing vouches for it. It is a driver: this program will receive the passwords of the connections that use it.".into(),
        }),
        ..Default::default()
    });
    let shown = paint(&mut model, 120, 30);
    assert!(shown.contains("this program will receive the passwords"), "{shown}");
    assert!(shown.contains("Type lake to install it"), "{shown}");
    insta::assert_snapshot!(shown);
}

#[test]
fn the_snapshot_lists_installed_plugins_without_the_index() {
    use dexo_app::plugin::{install, index::IndexSource};
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pack = dexo_test_support::plugins::theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    install::commit(data.path(), install::stage(data.path(), &pack).unwrap(), Origin::Local, None).unwrap();
    let offline = IndexSource {
        url: dexo_test_support::plugins::HttpServer::dead_url(),
        keys: vec![],
    };
    let snapshot = dexo_tui::runtime::plugin_manager::snapshot(data.path(), &offline, false);
    assert_eq!(snapshot.rows.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(snapshot.rows[0].provides, ["ocean"]);
    assert!(snapshot.index_error.is_some());
}
```

```rust
//! The Plugins screen's state -- what is installed, what the index offers, the one
//! change under way -- and its keys.

use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use dexo_app::plugin::manifest::Kind;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::{Installed, Origin};

use crate::action::Effect;
use crate::model::Model;
use crate::mouse::HitTarget;
use crate::screen::widgets::Search;
use crate::widgets::text_input::TextInput;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub enum PluginsView {
    #[default]
    Installed,
    Available,
}

impl PluginsView {
    pub const ALL: [Self; 2] = [Self::Installed, Self::Available];

    pub fn other(self) -> Self {
        match self {
            Self::Installed => Self::Available,
            Self::Available => Self::Installed,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub enum TypeFilter {
    #[default]
    All,
    Drivers,
    Themes,
}

impl TypeFilter {
    pub fn next(self) -> Self {
        match self {
            Self::All => Self::Drivers,
            Self::Drivers => Self::Themes,
            Self::Themes => Self::All,
        }
    }

    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::All => "all",
            Self::Drivers => "drivers",
            Self::Themes => "themes",
        }
    }

    pub fn admits(self, kind: Kind) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::All => true,
            Self::Drivers => kind == Kind::Driver,
            Self::Themes => kind == Kind::Theme,
        }
    }
}

/// One plugin as the screen lists it: installed, in the index, or both.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct PluginRow {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub description: String,
    pub authors: Vec<String>,
    pub license: String,
    pub installed: Option<Installed>,
    /// The newest version in the index this Dexo runs.
    pub newest: Option<String>,
    pub min_dexo: Option<String>,
    pub protocol: Option<u32>,
    /// Its download for this platform, in bytes.
    pub size: Option<u64>,
    /// Its drivers, or its themes' names.
    pub provides: Vec<String>,
    /// The first lines of its README.
    pub readme: Vec<String>,
}

impl PluginRow {
    /// The version an update would bring: an official plugin's, newer than installed.
    pub fn update(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        let installed = self.installed.as_ref()?;
        let newest = self.newest.as_deref()?;
        (installed.origin == Origin::Official
            && dexo_app::update::is_newer(newest, &installed.version))
        .then_some(newest)
    }

    /// Enabled, disabled, an update, available -- with a mark for each that reads
    /// without colour.
    pub fn mark(&self, unicode: bool) -> &'static str {
        let (enabled, disabled, update, available) = if unicode {
            ("●", "○", "↑", "+")
        } else {
            ("*", "o", "^", "+")
        };
        match &self.installed {
            None => available,
            Some(_) if self.update().is_some() => update,
            Some(installed) if installed.enabled => enabled,
            Some(_) => disabled,
        }
    }
}

/// What the screen shows, read off the disk and the index.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct PluginsSnapshot {
    pub rows: Vec<PluginRow>,
    /// Why there is no index, when there is none.
    pub index_error: Option<String>,
    /// The cache stood in for the index: what to say about it.
    pub offline: Option<String>,
}

/// An install or update under way.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Busy {
    pub id: String,
    pub verb: &'static str,
    pub done: u64,
    pub total: Option<u64>,
}

/// Installing from a file: its path typed, then -- the package read -- the trust
/// prompt, which typing the plugin's id answers.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct FromFile {
    pub path: TextInput,
    pub staged: Option<StagedInfo>,
    pub confirm: TextInput,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

/// A package read and waiting for trust; the runtime keeps it under `token`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct StagedInfo {
    pub token: u64,
    pub id: String,
    pub trust: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct PluginsState {
    pub view: PluginsView,
    pub snapshot: PluginsSnapshot,
    pub loading: bool,
    pub selected: usize,
    pub search: Search,
    pub filter: TypeFilter,
    pub busy: Option<Busy>,
    /// The plugin a removal is asked about.
    pub removing: Option<String>,
    pub from_file: Option<FromFile>,
}

impl PluginsState {
    /// The rows the view, the filter and the search let through.
    pub fn shown(&self) -> Vec<&PluginRow> {
        self.snapshot
            .rows
            .iter()
            .filter(|row| match self.view {
                PluginsView::Installed => row.installed.is_some(),
                PluginsView::Available => row.installed.is_none(),
            })
            .filter(|row| self.filter.admits(row.kind))
            .filter(|row| {
                self.search
                    .matches([row.id.as_str(), row.name.as_str(), row.description.as_str()])
            })
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn picked(&self) -> Option<&PluginRow> {
        let shown = self.shown();
        shown
            .get(self.selected.min(shown.len().saturating_sub(1)))
            .copied()
    }

    /// The installed plugins with an update: the count beside "Plugins" on the top line.
    pub fn updates(&self) -> usize {
        self.snapshot
            .rows
            .iter()
            .filter(|row| row.update().is_some())
            .count()
    }
}

/// The screen's keys. None leaves a key to the keymap, and Esc to going back.
pub fn handle_key(model: &mut Model, key: KeyEvent) -> Option<Vec<Effect>> {
    let state = &mut model.plugins;
    if state.search.typing && state.search.key(key) {
        state.selected = 0;
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    if key
        .modifiers
        .intersects(KeyModifiers::CONTROL | KeyModifiers::ALT)
    {
        return None;
    }
    let count = state.shown().len();
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Tab | KeyCode::BackTab => {
            state.view = state.view.other();
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('/') => state.search.typing = true,
        KeyCode::Char('t') => {
            state.filter = state.filter.next();
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('r') => {
            state.loading = true;
            return Some(vec![Effect::LoadPlugins { refresh: true }]);
        }
        KeyCode::Up => state.selected = state.selected.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Down => state.selected = (state.selected + 1).min(count.saturating_sub(1)),
        KeyCode::Home => state.selected = 0,
        KeyCode::End => state.selected = count.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Esc if !state.search.input.is_empty() || state.filter != TypeFilter::All => {
            state.search = Search::default();
            state.filter = TypeFilter::All;
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        _ => return None,
    }
    Some(Vec::new())
}

/// A click: a view, or a row.
pub fn mouse(model: &mut Model, hit: Option<HitTarget>) -> Vec<Effect> {
    match hit {
        Some(HitTarget::ScreenView(index)) => {
            if let Some(view) = PluginsView::ALL.get(index) {
                model.plugins.view = *view;
                model.plugins.selected = 0;
            }
        }
        Some(HitTarget::ListRow(index)) => model.plugins.selected = index,
        _ => {}
    }
    Vec::new()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The drawing** — `crates/dexo-tui/src/screen/plugins.rs`; add `pub mod plugins;` to `screen/mod.rs`.

```rust
//! Plugins: what is installed and what the index offers, the pick in full beside the
//! list with what can be done to it.

use crossterm::event::KeyCode;
use dexo_app::plugin::manifest::Kind;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::Origin;
use ratatui::Frame;
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::text::{Line, Text};
use ratatui::widgets::Paragraph;

use super::Button;
use super::widgets::{self, Chip, FieldRow};
use crate::model::Model;
use crate::mouse::HitMap;
use crate::screens::plugins::{FromFile, PluginRow, PluginsView, TypeFilter};
use crate::theme::Role;

pub fn render(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, model: &Model, hits: &mut HitMap) {
    let state = &model.plugins;
    let views = [
        ("Installed".to_string(), state.view == PluginsView::Installed),
        ("Available".to_string(), state.view == PluginsView::Available),
    ];
    let chips = [Chip {
        key: KeyCode::Char('t'),
        label: format!("Type: {}", state.filter.label()),
        active: state.filter != TypeFilter::All,
    }];
    let rest = super::views_and_toolbar(frame, area, model, hits, &views, Some(&state.search), &chips, &[]);
    let rest = notice(frame, rest, model);
    let rows = state.shown();
    if rows.is_empty() && state.from_file.is_none() {
        widgets::empty_with_buttons(frame, rest, model, hits, &empty_lines(model), &list_buttons(model));
        return;
    }
    let picked = state.selected.min(rows.len().saturating_sub(1));
    let (list, detail) = super::list_and_detail(model, hits, rest, rows.len() + 2);
    let width = rows
        .iter()
        .map(|row| row.name.chars().count())
        .max()
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .min(24);
    let unicode = model.capabilities.unicode;
    let lines: Vec<String> = rows.iter().map(|row| list_line(row, width, unicode)).collect();
    let title = match state.view {
        PluginsView::Installed => format!("Installed ({})", rows.len()),
        PluginsView::Available => format!("Available ({})", rows.len()),
    };
    super::list_pane(frame, list, model, hits, &title, None, &lines, (!rows.is_empty()).then_some(picked));
    let text_width = usize::from(detail.width.saturating_sub(2));
    let (title, text) = match (&state.from_file, &state.removing, rows.get(picked)) {
        (Some(from_file), _, _) => ("Install from file".to_string(), from_file_lines(model, from_file, text_width)),
        (None, Some(_), Some(row)) => (format!("Remove {}?", row.name), removal_lines(row)),
        (None, _, Some(row)) => (row.name.clone(), detail_lines(model, row, detail.width.saturating_sub(2))),
        (None, _, None) => return,
    };
    let drawn = super::detail_pane(
        frame,
        detail,
        model,
        hits,
        &title,
        &buttons(model),
        Text::from(text),
        usize::from(super::detail_scroll(model)),
        &footer(model),
    );
    if let Some(from_file) = &state.from_file {
        input_cursor(frame, drawn.body, from_file, text_width);
    }
}

/// Where a file's path or the plugin's id is being typed, the cursor.
fn input_cursor(frame: &mut Frame, body: Rect, from_file: &FromFile, width: usize) {
    let (row, input) = match &from_file.staged {
        None => (1, &from_file.path),
        Some(staged) => (from_file_lines_count(staged, width) - 1, &from_file.confirm),
    };
    if row < usize::from(body.height) {
        let line = Rect::new(body.x, body.y + row as u16, body.width, 1);
        crate::render::show_input(frame, line, "> ", input, false);
    }
}

/// The index's state over the list, when there is something to say.
fn notice(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, model: &Model) -> Rect {
    let snapshot = &model.plugins.snapshot;
    let (text, role) = match (&snapshot.index_error, &snapshot.offline) {
        (Some(error), _) => (error.clone(), Role::Error),
        (None, Some(offline)) => (offline.clone(), Role::Warning),
        _ if model.plugins.loading => ("Reading the plugin index…".to_string(), Role::Muted),
        _ => return area,
    };
    if area.height < 3 {
        return area;
    }
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(crate::model::truncate_cell(&text, usize::from(area.width)))
            .style(model.theme.style(role, model.capabilities)),
        Rect::new(area.x, area.y, area.width, 1),
    );
    Rect::new(area.x, area.y + 1, area.width, area.height - 1)
}

fn empty_lines(model: &Model) -> Vec<String> {
    let state = &model.plugins;
    if !state.search.input.is_empty() || state.filter != TypeFilter::All {
        return vec!["No plugin matches the search or the type.".into()];
    }
    match state.view {
        PluginsView::Installed => vec![
            "No plugins are installed.".into(),
            "Tab shows what the index offers: DuckDB, themes.".into(),
        ],
        PluginsView::Available if state.snapshot.index_error.is_some() => {
            vec!["The plugin index could not be had; Refresh tries again.".into()]
        }
        PluginsView::Available => vec!["Every plugin this Dexo runs is installed.".into()],
    }
}

/// `↑ DuckDB  driver  1.0.0  local`.
fn list_line(row: &PluginRow, width: usize, unicode: bool) -> String {
    let version = row
        .installed
        .as_ref()
        .map(|installed| installed.version.as_str())
        .or(row.newest.as_deref())
        .unwrap_or("");
    let local = if row
        .installed
        .as_ref()
        .is_some_and(|installed| installed.origin == Origin::Local)
    {
        "  local"
    } else {
        ""
    };
    format!(
        "{} {:<width$}  {:<6}  {version}{local}",
        row.mark(unicode),
        row.name,
        row.kind.as_str()
    )
}

fn megabytes(bytes: u64) -> String {
    format!("{:.1} MB", bytes as f64 / 1_048_576.0)
}

fn detail_lines(model: &Model, row: &PluginRow, width: u16) -> Vec<Line<'static>> {
    let mut fields = vec![FieldRow::Field("kind", row.kind.as_str().to_string())];
    if let Some(installed) = &row.installed {
        let disabled = if installed.enabled { "" } else { ", disabled" };
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("installed", format!("{}{disabled}", installed.version)));
    }
    if let Some(newest) = &row.newest {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("newest", newest.clone()));
    }
    if !row.authors.is_empty() {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("author", row.authors.join(", ")));
    }
    if !row.license.is_empty() {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("license", row.license.clone()));
    }
    if let Some(min_dexo) = &row.min_dexo {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("requires", format!("Dexo {min_dexo} or newer")));
    }
    if let Some(protocol) = row.protocol {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("protocol", protocol.to_string()));
    }
    if let Some(size) = row.size {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field("download", megabytes(size)));
    }
    let origin = match row.installed.as_ref().map(|installed| installed.origin) {
        Some(Origin::Local) => "local, not verified",
        _ => "official, signed",
    };
    fields.push(FieldRow::Field("origin", origin.into()));
    if !row.provides.is_empty() {
        let label = if row.kind == Kind::Driver { "drivers" } else { "themes" };
        fields.push(FieldRow::Field(label, row.provides.join(", ")));
    }
    if !row.description.is_empty() {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Blank);
        fields.push(FieldRow::Text(row.description.clone()));
    }
    if !row.readme.is_empty() {
        fields.push(FieldRow::Blank);
        fields.extend(row.readme.iter().map(|line| FieldRow::Text(line.clone())));
    }
    widgets::field_lines(model, &fields, width)
}

fn removal_lines(row: &PluginRow) -> Vec<Line<'static>> {
    let mut lines = vec![Line::raw(format!("Remove {} and its files?", row.name))];
    if row.kind == Kind::Driver {
        lines.push(Line::raw(
            "Connections that use its driver stay saved, and say it is not installed.",
        ));
    }
    lines.push(Line::default());
    lines.push(Line::raw("Enter removes it, Esc keeps it."));
    lines
}

fn from_file_lines(model: &Model, from_file: &FromFile, width: usize) -> Vec<Line<'static>> {
    let mut lines = vec![
        Line::raw("A plugin's .tar.gz, or its folder:"),
        Line::raw(format!("> {}", from_file.path.as_str())),
    ];
    if let Some(staged) = &from_file.staged {
        let warning = model.theme.style(Role::Warning, model.capabilities);
        lines.push(Line::default());
        for line in crate::model::wrap_words(&staged.trust, width.max(20)) {
            lines.push(Line::styled(line, warning));
        }
        lines.push(Line::default());
        lines.push(Line::raw(format!("Type {} to install it:", staged.id)));
        lines.push(Line::raw(format!("> {}", from_file.confirm.as_str())));
    }
    if let Some(error) = &from_file.error {
        lines.push(Line::default());
        lines.push(Line::styled(
            error.clone(),
            model.theme.style(Role::Error, model.capabilities),
        ));
    }
    lines
}

/// The rows the trust prompt takes up to its confirmation input, that one included.
fn from_file_lines_count(staged: &crate::screens::plugins::StagedInfo, width: usize) -> usize {
    2 + 1 + crate::model::wrap_words(&staged.trust, width.max(20)).len() + 1 + 2
}

/// Under the detail: the download under way, a bar with bytes and percent.
fn footer(model: &Model) -> Vec<String> {
    let Some(busy) = &model.plugins.busy else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let (full, empty) = if model.capabilities.unicode { ('█', '░') } else { ('#', '-') };
    let line = match busy.total {
        Some(total) if total > 0 => {
            let percent = (busy.done * 100 / total).min(100);
            let filled = (percent / 5) as usize;
            format!(
                "{} {} [{}{}] {} of {} · {percent}%  Esc cancels",
                busy.verb,
                busy.id,
                full.to_string().repeat(filled),
                empty.to_string().repeat(20 - filled),
                megabytes(busy.done),
                megabytes(total)
            )
        }
        _ => format!("{} {}… {}  Esc cancels", busy.verb, busy.id, megabytes(busy.done)),
    };
    vec![line]
}

/// What acts on the whole screen: the index fetched again, a package from a file.
pub fn list_buttons(model: &Model) -> Vec<Button> {
    let idle = model.plugins.busy.is_none();
    let wait = "Wait for the download to finish, or Esc to cancel it.";
    vec![
        Button::new(KeyCode::Char('r'), "Refresh").enabled_if(idle, wait),
        Button::new(KeyCode::Char('f'), "Install from file…").enabled_if(idle, wait),
    ]
}

/// What can be done to the pick.
pub fn buttons(model: &Model) -> Vec<Button> {
    let state = &model.plugins;
    if state.from_file.is_some() || state.removing.is_some() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let Some(row) = state.picked() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let idle = state.busy.is_none();
    let wait = "Wait for the download to finish, or Esc to cancel it.";
    let Some(installed) = &row.installed else {
        return vec![Button::new(KeyCode::Char('i'), "Install").enabled_if(idle, wait)];
    };
    let mut buttons = Vec::new();
    if let Some(newest) = row.update() {
        buttons.push(Button::new(KeyCode::Char('u'), format!("Update to {newest}")).enabled_if(idle, wait));
    }
    let toggle = if installed.enabled { "Disable" } else { "Enable" };
    buttons.push(Button::new(KeyCode::Char('e'), toggle).enabled_if(idle, wait));
    if row.kind == Kind::Theme && installed.enabled {
        buttons.push(Button::new(KeyCode::Char('a'), "Apply"));
    }
    buttons.push(Button::new(KeyCode::Char('x'), "Remove…").enabled_if(idle, wait));
    buttons
}

pub fn hints(model: &Model) -> String {
    let state = &model.plugins;
    if state.from_file.is_some() {
        return "Enter goes on  Esc cancels".into();
    }
    if state.removing.is_some() {
        return "Enter removes  Esc keeps it".into();
    }
    if state.busy.is_some() {
        return "Esc cancels the download".into();
    }
    "Up/Down pick  Tab view  / search  t type  Esc back".into()
}
```

```rust
//! The Plugins screen's work, off the loop that draws.

use std::path::Path;

use dexo_app::plugin::index::{self, IndexSource};
use dexo_app::plugin::{manifest, plugins_dir, state};

use crate::screens::plugins::{PluginRow, PluginsSnapshot};

/// What the screen shows: every installed plugin with what its manifest says, and the
/// index's plugins with their newest version this Dexo runs. The index is the cache
/// within the day unless `refresh`. The index's drivers not installed are announced,
/// so the connection form offers them.
pub fn snapshot(data_dir: &Path, source: &IndexSource, refresh: bool) -> PluginsSnapshot {
    let plugins = plugins_dir(data_dir);
    let mut rows: Vec<PluginRow> = state::load(&plugins)
        .plugins
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|(id, installed)| {
            let folder = plugins.join(&id);
            let manifest = manifest::read(&folder)?;
            Some(PluginRow {
                provides: manifest::provides(&manifest, &folder),
                readme: readme(&folder),
                name: manifest.name.clone(),
                kind: manifest.kind,
                description: manifest.description.clone(),
                authors: manifest.authors.clone(),
                license: manifest.license.clone(),
                min_dexo: Some(manifest.min_dexo.clone()),
                protocol: manifest.driver.as_ref().map(|driver| driver.protocol),
                installed: Some(installed),
                newest: None,
                size: None,
                id,
            })
        })
        .collect();
    let mut snapshot = PluginsSnapshot::default();
    match index::load(data_dir, source, refresh) {
        Ok(fetched) => {
            if fetched.offline {
                snapshot.offline = Some(index::offline_note(fetched.fetched_at));
            }
            index::announce_available(&fetched.index);
            let dexo = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");
            for plugin in &fetched.index.plugins {
                let Ok(choice) = index::choose(&fetched.index, &plugin.id, None, dexo, index::target()) else {
                    continue;
                };
                match rows.iter_mut().find(|row| row.id == plugin.id) {
                    Some(row) => {
                        row.newest = Some(choice.version.version.clone());
                        row.size = Some(choice.artifact.size);
                    }
                    None => rows.push(PluginRow {
                        id: plugin.id.clone(),
                        name: plugin.name.clone(),
                        kind: plugin.kind,
                        description: plugin.description.clone(),
                        authors: plugin.authors.clone(),
                        license: plugin.license.clone(),
                        installed: None,
                        newest: Some(choice.version.version.clone()),
                        min_dexo: Some(choice.version.min_dexo.clone()),
                        protocol: choice.version.driver.as_ref().map(|driver| driver.protocol),
                        size: Some(choice.artifact.size),
                        provides: choice
                            .version
                            .driver
                            .as_ref()
                            .map(|driver| driver.drivers.clone())
                            .unwrap_or_default(),
                        readme: Vec::new(),
                    }),
                }
            }
        }
        Err(error) => snapshot.index_error = Some(error.to_string()),
    }
    rows.sort_by_key(|row| row.name.to_lowercase());
    snapshot.rows = rows;
    snapshot
}

/// The first lines of a plugin's README.
fn readme(folder: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    std::fs::read_to_string(folder.join("README.md"))
        .map(|text| text.lines().take(12).map(str::to_string).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn update_disable_and_remove_send_their_effects() {
    let mut model = screen();
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('u'));
    assert!(
        matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::InstallPlugin { id, upgrade: true }] if id == "duckdb"),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    assert!(model.plugins.busy.is_some());
    // While it downloads the other actions wait, and Esc cancels it.
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('x'));
    assert!(model.plugins.removing.is_none());
    assert!(matches!(&press(&mut model, KeyCode::Esc)[..], [Effect::CancelPluginWork]));
    update(&mut model, Action::PluginProgress { done: 50, total: Some(100) });
    assert_eq!(model.plugins.busy.as_ref().unwrap().done, 50);
    update(&mut model, Action::PluginWorkDone(Ok("Updated DuckDB to 1.1.0.".into())));
    assert!(model.plugins.busy.is_none());
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('e'));
    assert!(
        matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::SetPluginEnabled { id, enabled: false }] if id == "duckdb"),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('x'));
    assert_eq!(model.plugins.removing.as_deref(), Some("duckdb"));
    assert!(paint(&mut model, 120, 30).contains("Remove DuckDB and its files?"));
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::RemovePlugin { id }] if id == "duckdb"), "{effects:?}");
}

    let mut model = screen();
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('f'));
    for ch in "/tmp/lake.tar.gz".chars() {
        press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char(ch));
    }
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(
        matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::StagePluginFile { path }] if path == "/tmp/lake.tar.gz"),

        &mut model,

            token: 7,

    }

    assert!(paint(&mut model, 120, 30).contains("Type lake to install it."));
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('e'));
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::CommitPluginFile { token: 7 }]), "{effects:?}");
    assert!(model.plugins.from_file.is_none() && model.plugins.busy.is_some());
}

#[test]
fn esc_on_the_trust_prompt_drops_the_package() {
    let mut model = screen();
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('f'));
    update(
        &mut model,
        Action::PluginFileStaged(Ok(StagedInfo {
            token: 3,
            id: "lake".into(),
            trust: "Lake is not from Dexo's signed index.".into(),
        })),
    );
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Esc);
    assert!(matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::DropPluginFile { token: 3 }]), "{effects:?}");
    assert!(model.plugins.from_file.is_none());
}
```

Replace `handle_key` whole. The action keys come first, while nothing borrows `model.plugins`; then the list's own keys, as in Task 16:

```rust
/// The screen's keys. None leaves a key to the keymap, and Esc to going back.
pub fn handle_key(model: &mut Model, key: KeyEvent) -> Option<Vec<Effect>> {
    // A question or a path being typed holds the keys.
    if model.plugins.removing.is_some() {
        return Some(removal_key(model, key));
    }
    if model.plugins.from_file.is_some() {
        return Some(from_file_key(model, key));
    }
    if model.plugins.busy.is_some() && key.code == KeyCode::Esc {
        return Some(vec![Effect::CancelPluginWork]);
    }
    if model.plugins.search.typing && model.plugins.search.key(key) {
        model.plugins.selected = 0;
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    if key
        .modifiers
        .intersects(KeyModifiers::CONTROL | KeyModifiers::ALT)
    {
        return None;
    }
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Char('i') => return Some(install(model, false)),
        KeyCode::Char('u') => return Some(install(model, true)),
        KeyCode::Char('e') => return Some(toggle(model)),
        KeyCode::Char('a') => {
            let id = model.plugins.picked()?.id.clone();
            return Some(crate::update::update(
                model,
                crate::action::Action::ApplyPluginTheme { id },
            ));
        }
        KeyCode::Char('x') | KeyCode::Delete => {
            let id = model
                .plugins
                .picked()
                .filter(|row| row.installed.is_some())
                .map(|row| row.id.clone());
            model.plugins.removing = id;
            return Some(Vec::new());
        }
        KeyCode::Char('f') => {
            model.plugins.from_file = Some(FromFile::default());
            model.sections[crate::model::Screen::Plugins.index()] =
                crate::screen::Section::Detail;
            return Some(Vec::new());
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    let state = &mut model.plugins;
    let count = state.shown().len();
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Tab | KeyCode::BackTab => {
            state.view = state.view.other();
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('/') => state.search.typing = true,
        KeyCode::Char('t') => {
            state.filter = state.filter.next();
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('r') => {
            state.loading = true;
            return Some(vec![Effect::LoadPlugins { refresh: true }]);
        }
        KeyCode::Up => state.selected = state.selected.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Down => state.selected = (state.selected + 1).min(count.saturating_sub(1)),
        KeyCode::Home => state.selected = 0,
        KeyCode::End => state.selected = count.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Esc if !state.search.input.is_empty() || state.filter != TypeFilter::All => {
            state.search = Search::default();
            state.filter = TypeFilter::All;
            state.selected = 0;
        }
        _ => return None,
    }
    Some(Vec::new())
}
```

The helpers:

```rust
fn install(model: &mut Model, upgrade: bool) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let Some(row) = model.plugins.picked() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    if (upgrade && row.update().is_none()) || (!upgrade && row.installed.is_some()) {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let (id, total) = (row.id.clone(), row.size);
    model.plugins.busy = Some(Busy {
        id: id.clone(),
        verb: if upgrade { "Updating" } else { "Installing" },
        done: 0,
        total,
    });
    vec![Effect::InstallPlugin { id, upgrade }]
}

fn toggle(model: &mut Model) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let Some(row) = model.plugins.picked() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let Some(installed) = &row.installed else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    vec![Effect::SetPluginEnabled {
        id: row.id.clone(),
        enabled: !installed.enabled,
    }]
}

fn removal_key(model: &mut Model, key: KeyEvent) -> Vec<Effect> {
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Enter => match model.plugins.removing.take() {
            Some(id) => vec![Effect::RemovePlugin { id }],
            None => Vec::new(),
        },
        KeyCode::Esc => {
            model.plugins.removing = None;
            Vec::new()
        }
        _ => Vec::new(),
    }
}

/// The path, then the plugin's id typed to trust it. Esc drops a package already read.
fn from_file_key(model: &mut Model, key: KeyEvent) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let Some(from_file) = model.plugins.from_file.as_mut() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let staged = from_file.staged.clone();
    match (key.code, staged) {
        (KeyCode::Esc, staged) => {
            model.plugins.from_file = None;
            staged
                .map(|staged| vec![Effect::DropPluginFile { token: staged.token }])
                .unwrap_or_default()
        }
        (KeyCode::Enter, None) => {
            let path = from_file.path.as_str().trim().to_string();
            if path.is_empty() {
                return Vec::new();
            }
            from_file.error = None;
            vec![Effect::StagePluginFile { path }]
        }
        (KeyCode::Enter, Some(staged)) => {
            if from_file.confirm.as_str().trim() != staged.id {
                from_file.error = Some(format!("Type {} to install it.", staged.id));
                return Vec::new();
            }
            model.plugins.from_file = None;
            model.plugins.busy = Some(Busy {
                id: staged.id,
                verb: "Installing",
                done: 0,
                total: None,
            });
            vec![Effect::CommitPluginFile { token: staged.token }]
        }
        (_, None) => {
            from_file.path.handle_key(key);
            Vec::new()
        }
        (_, Some(_)) => {
            from_file.confirm.handle_key(key);
            Vec::new()
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The actions** — in `crates/dexo-tui/src/action.rs`, add the effects and actions named in **Interfaces**, each with a one-line doc comment. In `crates/dexo-tui/src/update.rs`'s action match:

```rust
        Action::PluginProgress { done, total } => {
            if let Some(busy) = model.plugins.busy.as_mut() {
                busy.done = done;
                busy.total = total.or(busy.total);
            }
            Vec::new()
        }
        Action::PluginWorkDone(done) => {
            model.plugins.busy = None;
            // A theme pack added, changed or taken away changes the theme picker, and
            // the theme in use when its pack went.
            if let Ok(paths) = dexo_storage::AppPaths::discover() {
                load_user_themes(model, &paths.data_dir);
            }
            match done {
                Ok(message) => {
                    model.messages.info(message);
                    match model.plugins.then_connect.take() {
                        Some(profile) => connect_to(model, profile),
                        None => Vec::new(),
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    model.plugins.then_connect = None;
                    model.messages.error(error);
                    Vec::new()
                }
            }
        }
        Action::PluginFileStaged(staged) => {
            if let Some(from_file) = model.plugins.from_file.as_mut() {
                match staged {
                    Ok(staged) => from_file.staged = Some(staged),
                    Err(error) => from_file.error = Some(error),
                }
            }
            Vec::new()
        }
        Action::ApplyPluginTheme { id } => {
            if let Ok(paths) = dexo_storage::AppPaths::discover() {
                load_user_themes(model, &paths.data_dir);
            }
            let Some(theme) = model.user_themes.iter().find(|theme| theme.pack.as_deref() == Some(id.as_str())) else {
                model.messages.info(format!("{id} has no theme to apply."));
                return Vec::new();
            };
            model.settings.theme = theme.key();
            let name = theme.name.clone();
            rebuild_theme(model);
            persist_settings(model);
            model.messages.info(format!("Using {name}; Settings steps through the pack's others."));
            Vec::new()
        }
```

Append to `crates/dexo-tui/src/runtime/plugin_manager.rs`:

```rust
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;
use std::sync::{Arc, PoisonError};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use dexo_app::plugin::install;
use dexo_app::plugin::state::Origin;
use dexo_app::{AppError, DriverRegistry, ErrorCategory};
use dexo_storage::AppPaths;

use crate::action::Action;
use crate::screens::plugins::StagedInfo;

impl super::WorkbenchRuntime {
    /// Runs `work` on a blocking thread with the data folder and the drivers -- a
    /// driver plugin installed here is registered for the whole of Dexo at once -- says
    /// how it went, and reads the screen's state again.
    pub(super) fn plugin_work(
        &self,
        work: impl FnOnce(&Path, &DriverRegistry) -> Result<String, AppError> + Send + 'static,
    ) {
        let registry = self.drivers.clone();
        self.off_the_loop(move || {
            let Ok(paths) = AppPaths::discover() else {
                return Vec::new();
            };
            let done = work(&paths.data_dir, &registry).map_err(|error| error.to_string());
            vec![
                Action::PluginWorkDone(done),
                Action::PluginsLoaded(snapshot(&paths.data_dir, &IndexSource::from_env(), false)),
            ]
        });
    }

    pub(super) fn install_plugin(&mut self, id: String, upgrade: bool) {
        let cancel = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
        self.plugin_cancel = Some(cancel.clone());
        let progress_tx = self.action_tx.clone();
        self.plugin_work(move |data_dir, registry| {
            let mut last: Option<Instant> = None;
            // A tenth of a second between reports: the screen does not need more.
            let mut progress = |done, total| {
                if last.is_none_or(|at| at.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(100)) {
                    last = Some(Instant::now());
                    let _ = progress_tx.blocking_send(Action::PluginProgress { done, total });
                }
            };
            let source = IndexSource::from_env();
            if upgrade {
                Ok(
                    match install::upgrade(data_dir, &source, &id, Some(registry), &mut progress, &cancel)? {
                        Some(manifest) => format!("Updated {} to {}.", manifest.name, manifest.version),
                        None => format!("{id} is up to date."),
                    },
                )
            } else {
                let manifest = install::install_from_index(
                    data_dir, &source, &id, None, Some(registry), &mut progress, &cancel,
                )?;
                Ok(format!("Installed {} {}.", manifest.name, manifest.version))
            }
        });
    }

    pub(super) fn stage_plugin_file(&mut self, path: String) {
        self.next_staged += 1;
        let token = self.next_staged;
        let staged_plugins = self.staged_plugins.clone();
        self.off_the_loop(move || {
            let Ok(paths) = AppPaths::discover() else {
                return Vec::new();
            };
            let staged = install::stage(&paths.data_dir, &expand_home(&path))
                .map(|staged| {
                    let info = StagedInfo {
                        token,
                        id: staged.manifest.id.clone(),
                        trust: dexo_app::plugin::trust_text(&staged.manifest),
                    };
                    staged_plugins
                        .lock()
                        .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
                        .insert(token, staged);
                    info
                })
                .map_err(|error| error.to_string());
            vec![Action::PluginFileStaged(staged)]
        });
    }

    pub(super) fn commit_plugin_file(&mut self, token: u64) {
        let staged = self
            .staged_plugins
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
            .remove(&token);
        self.plugin_work(move |data_dir, registry| {
            let staged = staged.ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::new(ErrorCategory::Configuration, "the package is gone: choose it again")
            })?;
            let manifest = install::commit(data_dir, staged, Origin::Local, Some(registry))?;
            Ok(format!(
                "Installed {} {} (local, not verified).",
                manifest.name, manifest.version
            ))
        });
    }
}

/// `~/x` as the home folder's `x`: a path typed by hand.
fn expand_home(path: &str) -> PathBuf {
    match path.strip_prefix("~/") {
        Some(rest) => std::env::var_os("HOME")
            .or_else(|| std::env::var_os("USERPROFILE"))
            .map_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(path), |home| PathBuf::from(home).join(rest)),
        None => PathBuf::from(path),
    }
}
```

`install::stage`, `commit` and `install_from_index` build their own current-thread runtime for a driver's handshake. That is why they run in `off_the_loop`'s blocking thread and never in an async task.

```rust
//! A driver that is a plugin not installed is offered where it is wanted: opening a
//! saved connection on it, or testing a new one. Each test makes up its own driver, as
//! the drivers Dexo knows are shared by the whole test process.
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use dexo_app::{ConnectionId, ConnectionProfile, SecretRef};
use dexo_driver_api::DriverDescriptor;
use dexo_tui::mouse::HitMap;
use dexo_tui::{Action, Effect, Model, update};

fn paint(model: &mut Model) -> String {
    let mut terminal = ratatui::Terminal::new(ratatui::backend::TestBackend::new(120, 36)).unwrap();
    let mut hits = HitMap::default();
    terminal
        .draw(|frame| dexo_tui::render::render(frame, model, &mut hits))
        .unwrap();
    model.hits = hits;
    dexo_tui::render::render_to_string(model, 120, 36)
}

fn press(model: &mut Model, code: KeyCode) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let effects = update(model, Action::Key(KeyEvent::new(code, KeyModifiers::NONE)));
    paint(model);
    effects
}

#[test]
fn opening_a_connection_whose_driver_is_not_installed_offers_its_plugin() {
    DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver("warehouse", "Warehouse", true, "warehouse", false));
    let mut model = Model::default();
    model.connections.load_profiles(vec![ConnectionProfile::new(
        ConnectionId(uuid::Uuid::new_v4()),
        None,
        "lake",
        "warehouse",
        "local",
        serde_json::json!({"path": "/tmp/lake.wh"}),
        SecretRef::new("ref-lake"),
    )]);
    update(&mut model, Action::GoToScreen(dexo_tui::model::Screen::Connections));
    paint(&mut model);
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Char('s'));
    assert!(
        !effects.iter().any(|effect| matches!(effect, Effect::ConnectProfile { .. })),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    let shown = paint(&mut model);
    assert!(shown.contains("Warehouse is a plugin that is not installed."), "{shown}");
    let effects = press(&mut model, KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(
        matches!(&effects[..], [Effect::InstallPlugin { id, upgrade: false }] if id == "warehouse"),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    // Installed: the runtime's registry says so, and the connection opens.
    DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver("warehouse", "Warehouse", true, "warehouse", true));
    let effects = update(&mut model, Action::PluginWorkDone(Ok("Installed Warehouse 1.0.0.".into())));
    assert!(
        effects
            .iter()
            .any(|effect| matches!(effect, Effect::ConnectProfile { profile, .. } if profile.name == "lake")),
        "{effects:?}"
    );
    DriverDescriptor::withdraw("warehouse");
}

#[test]
fn testing_a_new_connection_on_a_driver_not_installed_offers_it_and_keeps_the_form() {
    DriverDescriptor::announce(DriverDescriptor::plugin_driver("lakehouse", "Lakehouse", true, "lakehouse", false));
    let mut model = Model::default();
    model.apply_size(120, 36);
    update(&mut model, Action::OpenConnectionForm);
    let driver = model
        .connection_form
        .fields
        .iter_mut()
        .find(|field| field.label == "driver")
        .unwrap();
    driver.value = dexo_tui::widgets::text_input::TextInput::new("lakehouse");
    let effects = update(&mut model, Action::TestConnection);
    assert!(effects.is_empty(), "{effects:?}");
    let offer = model.plugin_offer.clone().expect("an offer");
    assert_eq!((offer.plugin.as_str(), offer.driver.as_str()), ("lakehouse", "Lakehouse"));
    press(&mut model, KeyCode::Esc);
    assert!(model.plugin_offer.is_none() && model.connection_form.open);
    DriverDescriptor::withdraw("lakehouse");
}
```

```rust
/// A plugin offered where its driver was wanted: the connection form, or a saved
/// connection being opened.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct PluginOffer {
    pub plugin: String,
    /// The driver as people name it.
    pub driver: String,
    pub focus: crate::widgets::form::FooterFocus,
    /// The connection to open once it is installed.
    pub then_connect: Option<dexo_app::ConnectionProfile>,
}

/// The plugin to install for `driver`, and the driver's name, when it is a plugin's
/// driver that is not installed.
pub fn missing_driver(driver: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    let descriptor = dexo_driver_api::DriverDescriptor::for_id(driver.trim())?;
    if descriptor.installed {
        return None;
    }
    Some((descriptor.plugin?.into_owned(), descriptor.display_name.into_owned()))
}
```

```rust
/// The offer of a missing driver's plugin: Enter installs it, Esc leaves things as
/// they were.
fn handle_plugin_offer_key(model: &mut Model, key: KeyEvent) -> Vec<Effect> {
    use crate::widgets::form::{FooterKey, confirm_key};
    let Some(offer) = model.plugin_offer.as_mut() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    match confirm_key(&mut offer.focus, &key) {
        FooterKey::Submit => accept_plugin_offer(model),
        FooterKey::Cancel => {
            model.plugin_offer = None;
            Vec::new()
        }
        FooterKey::Moved | FooterKey::Pass => Vec::new(),
    }
}

fn mouse_plugin_offer(model: &mut Model, hit: Option<HitTarget>) -> Vec<Effect> {
    match hit {
        Some(HitTarget::FooterSubmit) => accept_plugin_offer(model),
        Some(HitTarget::FooterCancel) => {
            model.plugin_offer = None;
            Vec::new()
        }
        _ => Vec::new(),
    }
}

/// Installs the offered plugin; the connection that wanted it opens once it is in.
fn accept_plugin_offer(model: &mut Model) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let Some(offer) = model.plugin_offer.take() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    model.plugins.busy = Some(crate::screens::plugins::Busy {
        id: offer.plugin.clone(),
        verb: "Installing",
        done: 0,
        total: None,
    });
    model.plugins.then_connect = offer.then_connect;
    model
        .messages
        .info(format!("Installing the {} plugin…", offer.driver));
    vec![Effect::InstallPlugin {
        id: offer.plugin,
        upgrade: false,
    }]
}
```

```rust
fn render_plugin_offer(
    frame: &mut Frame,
    model: &Model,
    offer: &crate::screens::plugins::PluginOffer,
    hits: &mut HitMap,
) {
    use crate::widgets::form::footer_line;
    let area = frame.area();
    if area.width < 10 || area.height < 5 {
        return;
    }
    let lines = vec![
        format!("{} is a plugin that is not installed.", offer.driver),
        "Install it from Dexo's signed plugin index?".to_string(),
        String::new(),
        footer_line("Install", offer.focus),
    ];
    let width = lines
        .iter()
        .map(|line| line.chars().count() as u16 + 4)
        .max()
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .max(40);
    let popup = centered(area, width, lines.len() as u16 + 2);
    paint_popup(frame, model, popup, overlay_block(model, "Install plugin?"), lines.join("\n"));
    register_overlay(hits, popup);
    let footer = crate::mouse::line_rect(popup_inner(popup), lines.len() - 1);
    crate::widgets::form::register_footer(hits, footer, &lines[lines.len() - 1], "Install");
}
```

`crates/dexo-tui/src/runtime/update_check.rs` — the daily check reads the plugin index too. It honours the same setting and variable, and uses the day-old cache:

```rust
pub fn spawn(data_dir: PathBuf, action_tx: tokio::sync::mpsc::Sender<Action>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        if !allowed(&data_dir) {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(action) = check(&data_dir).await {
            let _ = action_tx.send(action).await;
        }
        // The installed plugins' updates, counted beside "Plugins"; and the index's
        // drivers, offered in the connection form.
        let plugins = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
            crate::runtime::plugin_manager::snapshot(
                &data_dir,
                &dexo_app::plugin::index::IndexSource::from_env(),
                false,
            )
        })
        .await;
        if let Ok(snapshot) = plugins {
            let _ = action_tx.send(Action::PluginsLoaded(snapshot)).await;
        }
    });
}

/// Off when the user turned it off, or when `DEXO_NO_UPDATE_CHECK` is set.
fn allowed(data_dir: &Path) -> bool {
    std::env::var_os("DEXO_NO_UPDATE_CHECK").is_none()
        && dexo_app::settings::load_settings(data_dir).update_check
}
```

Remove the same two conditions from the start of `check`.

```rust
//! The plugin's manifest and the crate agree: the release checks the tag against both.
#[test]
fn the_manifest_names_this_crate_and_its_version() {
    let path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../plugins/duckdb/dexo-plugin.toml");
    let manifest = dexo_app::plugin::manifest::parse(&std::fs::read_to_string(path).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(manifest.id, "duckdb");
    assert_eq!(manifest.version, env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
    let driver = manifest.driver.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(driver.executable, "bin/dexo-driver-duckdb");
    assert_eq!(driver.drivers, ["duckdb"]);
}
```

In `crates/dexo-tui/src/theme.rs`'s tests:

```rust
    #[test]
    fn the_extra_themes_pack_is_six_themes_that_parse() {
        let pack = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../plugins/extra-themes");
        let manifest = dexo_app::plugin::manifest::parse(
            &std::fs::read_to_string(pack.join("dexo-plugin.toml")).unwrap(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(manifest.id, "extra-themes");
        let mut count = 0;
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(pack.join("themes")).unwrap() {
            let path = entry.unwrap().path();
            super::parse_theme(&std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap())
                .unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("{}: {error}", path.display()));
            count += 1;
        }
        assert_eq!(count, 6);
    }
```

`crates/dexo-app/tests/plugin_index_script.rs`:

```rust
//! The release's index script writes an index Dexo reads, listing a package as it is,
//! and refuses a version already listed. Linux only: the script needs Python 3.11.
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]

use std::path::Path;
use std::process::Command;

use dexo_test_support::plugins::{pack, theme_folder};

fn run(current: &Path, manifest: &Path, dist: &Path) -> std::process::Output {
    let script = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../scripts/plugin-index.py");
    Command::new("python3")
        .arg(script)
        .arg(current)
        .arg(manifest)
        .arg(dist)
        .arg("https://example.invalid/r")
        .output()
        .expect("python3")
}

#[test]
fn the_release_script_writes_an_index_dexo_reads() {
    let work = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dist = work.path().join("dist");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dist).unwrap();
    let folder = theme_folder(work.path(), "pack", "1.0.0");
    let bytes = pack(&folder);
    std::fs::write(dist.join("pack-1.0.0.tar.gz"), &bytes).unwrap();
    let manifest = folder.join("dexo-plugin.toml");
    let first = run(&work.path().join("missing.json"), &manifest, &dist);
    assert!(first.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&first.stderr));
    let index: dexo_app::plugin::index::Index = serde_json::from_slice(&first.stdout).unwrap();
    let artifact = &index.plugins[0].versions[0].artifacts["any"];
    assert_eq!(artifact.size, bytes.len() as u64);
    assert_eq!(artifact.url, "https://example.invalid/r/pack-1.0.0.tar.gz");
    let current = work.path().join("index.json");
    std::fs::write(&current, &first.stdout).unwrap();
    let again = run(&current, &manifest, &dist);
    assert!(!again.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&again.stderr).contains("already in the index"));
}
```

`plugins/duckdb/README.md`:

```markdown
# DuckDB for Dexo

Opens a DuckDB file, `:memory:`, or a CSV, Parquet or JSON file read as a table.

- From the Plugins screen (`Ctrl+G p`), or `dexo plugin install duckdb`.
- Then add a connection with the DuckDB driver and a path, or run `dexo duckdb:///path/to/file.parquet`.

The engine runs in its own process, started on the first connection and stopped a minute after the last one closes.
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The pack and index scripts**

`scripts/pack-plugin.sh` (`chmod +x`):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Pack a plugin's .tar.gz from plugins/<id>: its manifest, the licence and its README,
# and the driver's executable under bin/ or the theme files under themes/.
#
#   scripts/pack-plugin.sh <id> <version> <target> [executable]
#
# A driver passes its target triple and executable; a theme pack passes `any`. The
# archive lands in dist/; its name, sha256 and size are printed.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

die() {
  printf 'pack-plugin: %s\n' "$*" >&2
  exit 1
}

id=${1:-} version=${2:-} target=${3:-} executable=${4:-}
[[ -n $id && -n $version && -n $target ]] ||
  die "usage: scripts/pack-plugin.sh <id> <version> <target|any> [executable]"
source_dir=plugins/$id
[[ -f $source_dir/dexo-plugin.toml ]] || die "no $source_dir/dexo-plugin.toml"
grep -qx "version = \"$version\"" "$source_dir/dexo-plugin.toml" ||
  die "$source_dir/dexo-plugin.toml is not at $version"

stage=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$stage"' EXIT
cp "$source_dir/dexo-plugin.toml" "$stage/"
cat LICENSE-MIT <(printf '\n') LICENSE-APACHE >"$stage/LICENSE"
[[ -f $source_dir/README.md ]] && cp "$source_dir/README.md" "$stage/"
if [[ -n $executable ]]; then
  mkdir "$stage/bin"
  cp "$executable" "$stage/bin/"
  name=$id-$version-$target.tar.gz
else
  [[ $target == any ]] || die "a theme pack's target is any"
  mkdir "$stage/themes"
  cp "$source_dir"/themes/*.toml "$stage/themes/"
  name=$id-$version.tar.gz
fi
mkdir -p dist
# Entries named plainly -- no `./` -- and no macOS resource forks: Dexo refuses any
# file a plugin does not ship.
(cd "$stage" && COPYFILE_DISABLE=1 tar -czf - -- *) >"dist/$name"
sha=$( (sha256sum "dist/$name" 2>/dev/null || shasum -a 256 "dist/$name") | cut -d' ' -f1)
size=$(wc -c <"dist/$name" | tr -d ' ')
printf '%s %s %s\n' "$name" "$sha" "$size"
```

`scripts/plugin-index.py` (`chmod +x`):

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Add one plugin release to Dexo's plugin index, written to stdout.

    python3 scripts/plugin-index.py <current index.json> <dexo-plugin.toml> <dist dir> <release url>

The current index may be missing: the first release starts one. Every
<id>-<version>*.tar.gz in <dist dir> is listed with its sha256 and size. Refused: an
index of another schema, a manifest Dexo would refuse, a version already listed, an
artifact for a platform Dexo is not built for.
"""
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import tomllib

TARGETS = {
    "x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu",
    "aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu",
    "x86_64-apple-darwin",
    "aarch64-apple-darwin",
    "x86_64-pc-windows-msvc",
}
ID = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{1,31}$")
VERSION = re.compile(r"^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$")
MANIFEST_KEYS = {"schema", "id", "kind", "version", "name", "description", "authors",
                 "license", "homepage", "min_dexo", "driver", "theme"}
DRIVER_KEYS = {"protocol", "drivers", "display_name", "dialect", "file", "url_schemes", "executable"}
LISTED_DRIVER_KEYS = ("protocol", "drivers", "display_name", "dialect", "file", "url_schemes")


def die(message):
    print(f"plugin-index: {message}", file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)


def read_manifest(path):
    with open(path, "rb") as file:
        manifest = tomllib.load(file)
    unknown = set(manifest) - MANIFEST_KEYS
    if unknown:
        die(f"unknown keys in the manifest: {sorted(unknown)}")
    if manifest.get("schema") != 1:
        die("the manifest is not schema 1")
    if not ID.match(manifest.get("id", "")):
        die(f"{manifest.get('id')!r} is not a plugin id")
    for field in ("version", "min_dexo"):
        if not VERSION.match(manifest.get(field, "")):
            die(f"{field} {manifest.get(field)!r} is not a version")
    kind = manifest.get("kind")
    if kind not in ("driver", "theme"):
        die(f"unknown kind {kind!r}")
    if ("driver" in manifest) != (kind == "driver") or ("theme" in manifest) != (kind == "theme"):
        die("the manifest's section does not match its kind")
    if kind == "driver" and set(manifest["driver"]) - DRIVER_KEYS:
        die(f"unknown [driver] keys: {sorted(set(manifest['driver']) - DRIVER_KEYS)}")
    return manifest


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) != 5:
        die("usage: plugin-index.py <current index.json> <dexo-plugin.toml> <dist dir> <release url>")
    current, manifest_path, dist, release_url = sys.argv[1:]
    index = {"schema": 1, "generated": "", "plugins": []}
    if os.path.exists(current):
        with open(current, encoding="utf-8") as file:
            index = json.load(file)
    if index.get("schema") != 1:
        die("the current index is not schema 1")
    manifest = read_manifest(manifest_path)
    plugin_id, kind, version = manifest["id"], manifest["kind"], manifest["version"]
    entry = {"version": version, "min_dexo": manifest["min_dexo"], "artifacts": {}}
    if kind == "driver":
        entry["driver"] = {key: manifest["driver"][key] for key in LISTED_DRIVER_KEYS if key in manifest["driver"]}
    prefix = f"{plugin_id}-{version}"
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(dist)):
        if not (name.startswith(prefix) and name.endswith(".tar.gz")):
            continue
        target = name[len(prefix):-len(".tar.gz")].lstrip("-") or "any"
        if kind == "driver" and target not in TARGETS:
            die(f"{name}: {target!r} is not a platform Dexo is built for")
        if kind == "theme" and target != "any":
            die(f"{name}: a theme pack has one artifact, for any platform")
        with open(os.path.join(dist, name), "rb") as file:
            data = file.read()
        entry["artifacts"][target] = {
            "url": f"{release_url}/{name}",
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
            "size": len(data),
        }
    if not entry["artifacts"]:
        die(f"no {prefix}*.tar.gz in {dist}")
    plugin = next((p for p in index["plugins"] if p["id"] == plugin_id), None)
    if plugin is None:
        plugin = {"id": plugin_id, "versions": []}
        index["plugins"].append(plugin)
    if plugin.get("kind", kind) != kind:
        die(f"{plugin_id} was a {plugin['kind']} plugin")
    if any(listed["version"] == version for listed in plugin["versions"]):
        die(f"{plugin_id} {version} is already in the index")
    for key in ("kind", "name", "description", "license", "homepage"):
        plugin[key] = manifest.get(key, "")
    plugin["authors"] = manifest.get("authors", [])
    plugin["versions"].append(entry)
    index["plugins"].sort(key=lambda listed: listed["id"])
    index["generated"] = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    json.dump(index, sys.stdout, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
    sys.stdout.write("\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
